===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.extractExpression(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 440-508
Documentation:
/**
   * @param expr The expression to extract.
   * @param injectionPoint The node before which to added the extracted
   *     expression.
   * @return The extract statement node.
   */
Method code:
440 |   private Node extractExpression(Node expr, Node injectionPoint) {
441 |     Node parent = expr.getParent();
442 | 
443 |     boolean isLhsOfAssignOp = NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent)
444 |         && !parent.isAssign()
445 |         && parent.getFirstChild() == expr;
446 | 
447 |     Node firstExtractedNode = null;
448 | 
449 |     // Expressions on the LHS of an assignment-op must have any possible
450 |     // side-effects extracted as the value must be duplicated:
451 |     //    next().foo += 2;
452 |     // becomes:
453 |     //    var t1 = next();
454 |     //    t1.foo = t1.foo + 2;
455 |     if (isLhsOfAssignOp && NodeUtil.isGet(expr)) {
456 |       for (Node n : expr.children()) {
457 |         if (!n.isString() && !isConstantName(n, knownConstants)) {
458 |           Node extractedNode = extractExpression(n, injectionPoint);
459 |           if (firstExtractedNode == null) {
460 |             firstExtractedNode = extractedNode;
461 |           }
462 |         }
463 |       }
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     // The temp is known to be constant.
467 |     String tempName = getTempConstantValueName();
468 |     Node replacementValueNode = IR.name(tempName).srcref(expr);
469 | 
470 |     Node tempNameValue;
471 | 
472 |     // If it is ASSIGN_XXX, keep the assignment in place and extract the
473 |     // original value of the LHS operand.
474 |     if (isLhsOfAssignOp) {
475 |       Preconditions.checkState(expr.isName() || NodeUtil.isGet(expr));
476 |       // Transform "x += 2" into "x = temp + 2"
477 |       Node opNode = new Node(NodeUtil.getOpFromAssignmentOp(parent))
478 |           .copyInformationFrom(parent);
479 | 
480 |       Node rightOperand = parent.getLastChild();
481 | 
482 |       parent.setType(Token.ASSIGN);
483 |       parent.replaceChild(rightOperand, opNode);
484 |       opNode.addChildToFront(replacementValueNode);
485 |       opNode.addChildToBack(rightOperand);
486 | 
487 |       // The original expression is still being used, so make a clone.
488 |       tempNameValue = expr.cloneTree();
489 |     } else {
490 |       // Replace the expression with the temporary name.
491 |       parent.replaceChild(expr, replacementValueNode);
492 | 
493 |       // Keep the original node so that CALL expressions can still be found
494 |       // and inlined properly.
495 |       tempNameValue = expr;
496 |     }
497 | 
498 |     // Re-add the expression in the declaration of the temporary name.
499 |     Node tempVarNode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(tempName, tempNameValue);
500 | 
501 |     Node injectionPointParent = injectionPoint.getParent();
502 |     injectionPointParent.addChildBefore(tempVarNode, injectionPoint);
503 | 
504 |     if (firstExtractedNode == null) {
505 |       firstExtractedNode = tempVarNode;
506 |     }
507 |     return firstExtractedNode;
508 |   }

Method code:
470 |     @Override
471 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
472 |       if (lineBreak) {
473 |         if (sawFunction) {
474 |           startNewLine();
475 |           sawFunction = false;
476 |         }
477 |       }
478 | 
479 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
480 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
481 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
482 |       int len = code.length();
483 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
484 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
485 |         if (ch == ';') {
486 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
487 |         }
488 |       }
489 |       maybeCutLine();
490 |     }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 610-675
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * - Don't inline if the calling function contains an inner function and
   * inlining would introduce new globals.
   */
Method code:
610 |   private boolean callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(
611 |       NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, final Node fnNode,
612 |       Set<String> namesToAlias) {
613 |     final boolean assumeMinimumCapture = this.assumeMinimumCapture;
614 | 
615 |     // Note: functions that contain function definitions are filtered out
616 |     // in isCandidateFunction.
617 | 
618 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Determining if the called function contains VARs
619 |     // or if the caller contains inner functions accounts for 20% of the
620 |     // run-time cost of this pass.
621 | 
622 |     // Don't inline functions with var declarations into a scope with inner
623 |     // functions as the new vars would leak into the inner function and
624 |     // cause memory leaks.
625 |     boolean fnContainsVars = NodeUtil.has(
626 |         NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode),
627 |         new NodeUtil.MatchDeclaration(),
628 |         new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
629 |     boolean forbidTemps = false;
630 |     if (!t.inGlobalScope()) {
631 |       Node fnCaller = t.getScopeRoot();
632 |       Node fnCallerBody = fnCaller.getLastChild();
633 | 
634 |       // Don't allow any new vars into a scope that contains eval or one
635 |       // that contains functions (excluding the function being inlined).
636 |       Predicate<Node> match = new Predicate<Node>(){
637 |         @Override
638 |         public boolean apply(Node n) {
639 |           if (n.isName()) {
640 |             return n.getString().equals("eval");
641 |           }
642 |           if (!assumeMinimumCapture && n.isFunction()) {
643 |             return n != fnNode;
644 |           }
645 |           return false;
646 |         }
647 |       };
648 |       forbidTemps = NodeUtil.has(fnCallerBody,
649 |           match, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION);
650 |     }
651 | 
652 |     if (fnContainsVars && forbidTemps) {
653 |       return false;
654 |     }
655 | 
656 |     // If the caller contains functions or evals, verify we aren't adding any
657 |     // additional VAR declarations because aliasing is needed.
658 |     if (forbidTemps) {
659 |       Map<String, Node> args =
660 |           FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(
661 |               fnNode, callNode, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
662 |       boolean hasArgs = !args.isEmpty();
663 |       if (hasArgs) {
664 |         // Limit the inlining
665 |         Set<String> allNamesToAlias = Sets.newHashSet(namesToAlias);
666 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(
667 |             fnNode, args, allNamesToAlias, compiler.getCodingConvention());
668 |         if (!allNamesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
669 |           return false;
670 |         }
671 |       }
672 |     }
673 | 
674 |     return true;
675 |   }

===== M0029 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineReturnValue(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 256-287
Documentation:
/**
   * Inline a function that fulfills the requirements of
   * canInlineReferenceDirectly into the call site, replacing only the CALL
   * node.
   */
Method code:
256 |   private Node inlineReturnValue(Node callNode, Node fnNode) {
257 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
258 |     Node callParentNode = callNode.getParent();
259 | 
260 |     // NOTE: As the normalize pass guarantees globals aren't being
261 |     // shadowed and an expression can't introduce new names, there is
262 |     // no need to check for conflicts.
263 | 
264 |     // Create an argName -> expression map, checking for side effects.
265 |     Map<String, Node> argMap =
266 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(
267 |             fnNode, callNode, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
268 | 
269 |     Node newExpression;
270 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
271 |       Node srcLocation = block;
272 |       newExpression = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation);
273 |     } else {
274 |       Node returnNode = block.getFirstChild();
275 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(returnNode.isReturn());
276 | 
277 |       // Clone the return node first.
278 |       Node safeReturnNode = returnNode.cloneTree();
279 |       Node inlineResult = FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(
280 |           null, safeReturnNode, null, argMap);
281 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(safeReturnNode == inlineResult);
282 |       newExpression = safeReturnNode.removeFirstChild();
283 |     }
284 | 
285 |     callParentNode.replaceChild(callNode, newExpression);
286 |     return newExpression;
287 |   }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.moveExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 112-129
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract the specified expression from its parent expression.
   * @see #canExposeExpression
   */
Method code:
112 |   void moveExpression(Node expression) {
113 |     String resultName = getResultValueName();
114 |     Node injectionPoint = findInjectionPoint(expression);
115 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(injectionPoint);
116 |     Node injectionPointParent = injectionPoint.getParent();
117 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(injectionPointParent);
118 |     Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(injectionPointParent));
119 | 
120 |     // Replace the expression with a reference to the new name.
121 |     Node expressionParent = expression.getParent();
122 |     expressionParent.replaceChild(
123 |         expression, IR.name(resultName));
124 | 
125 |     // Re-add the expression at the appropriate place.
126 |     Node newExpressionRoot = NodeUtil.newVarNode(resultName, expression);
127 |     injectionPointParent.addChildBefore(newExpressionRoot, injectionPoint);
128 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
129 |   }

1785 |   static boolean isStatementParent(Node parent) {
1786 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1787 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1788 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.

1699 |   public Node cloneNode() {

1713 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 552-572
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

===== M0071 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 210-233
Documentation:
/**
     * Find function expressions that are called directly in the form of
     *   (function(a,b,...){...})(a,b,...)
     * or
     *   (function(a,b,...){...}).call(this,a,b, ...)
     */
Method code:
210 |     public void findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
211 |       switch (n.getType()) {
212 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
213 |         //   (function(){})();
214 |         case Token.CALL:
215 |           Node fnNode = null;
216 |           if (n.getFirstChild().isFunction()) {
217 |             fnNode = n.getFirstChild();
218 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
219 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
220 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.isFunction()) {
221 |               fnNode = fnIdentifingNode;
222 |             }
223 |           }
224 | 
225 |           // If a interesting function was discovered, add it.
226 |           if (fnNode != null) {
227 |             Function fn = new FunctionExpression(fnNode, callsSeen++);
228 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
229 |             anonFns.put(fnNode, fn.getName());
230 |           }
231 |           break;
232 |       }
233 |     }

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.estimateCallCost(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 860-878
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets an estimate of the cost in characters of making the function call:
   * the sum of the identifiers and the separators.
   * @param referencesThis
   */
Method code:
860 |   private static int estimateCallCost(Node fnNode, boolean referencesThis) {
861 |     Node argsNode = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode);
862 |     int numArgs = argsNode.getChildCount();
863 | 
864 |     int callCost = NAME_COST_ESTIMATE + PAREN_COST;
865 |     if (numArgs > 0) {
866 |       callCost += (numArgs * NAME_COST_ESTIMATE) + ((numArgs - 1) * COMMA_COST);
867 |     }
868 | 
869 |     if (referencesThis) {
870 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Update this if we start supporting inlining
871 |       // other functions that reference this.
872 |       // The only functions that reference this that are currently inlined
873 |       // are those that are called via ".call" with an explicit "this".
874 |       callCost += 5 + 5;  // ".call" + "this,"
875 |     }
876 | 
877 |     return callCost;
878 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 1180-1216
Documentation:

Method code:
1180 |     @Override
1181 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
1182 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
1183 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
1184 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.isNumber()) {
1185 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());
1186 |         return operand;
1187 |       } else {
1188 |         if (type == Token.DELPROP &&
1189 |             !(operand.isGetProp() ||
1190 |               operand.isGetElem() ||
1191 |               operand.isName())) {
1192 |           String msg =
1193 |               "Invalid delete operand. Only properties can be deleted.";
1194 |           errorReporter.error(
1195 |               msg,
1196 |               sourceName,
1197 |               operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
1198 |         } else  if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1199 |           if (!validAssignmentTarget(operand)) {
1200 |             String msg = (type == Token.INC)
1201 |                 ? "invalid increment target"
1202 |                 : "invalid decrement target";
1203 |             errorReporter.error(
1204 |                 msg,
1205 |                 sourceName,
1206 |                 operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
1207 |           }
1208 |         }
1209 | 
1210 |         Node node = newNode(type, operand);
1211 |         if (exprNode.isPostfix()) {
1212 |           node.putBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, true);
1213 |         }
1214 |         return node;
1215 |       }
1216 |     }

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
422 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
423 |       Preconditions.checkState(
424 |           n.isName()
425 |           || n.isString()
426 |           || n.isStringKey()
427 |           || n.isGetterDef()
428 |           || n.isSetterDef());
429 | 
430 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
431 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
432 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
433 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n);
434 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
435 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
436 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
437 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
438 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
439 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
440 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
441 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
442 |           if (assertOnChange) {
443 |             String name = n.getString();
444 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
445 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
446 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
447 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
448 |           }
449 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silenceThreshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0118 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.findInjectionPoint(Node)

Original range: 621-636
Documentation:
/**
   * @return For the subExpression, find the nearest statement Node before which
   * it can be inlined.  Null if no such location can be found.
   */
Method code:
621 |   static Node findInjectionPoint(Node subExpression) {
622 |     Node expressionRoot = findExpressionRoot(subExpression);
623 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(expressionRoot);
624 | 
625 |     Node injectionPoint = expressionRoot;
626 | 
627 |     Node parent = injectionPoint.getParent();
628 |     while (parent.isLabel()) {

630 |       parent = injectionPoint.getParent();

635 |     return injectionPoint;

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.CallVisitor.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 386-418
Documentation:

Method code:
386 |     @Override
387 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
388 |       switch (n.getType()) {
389 |         // Function calls
390 |         case Token.CALL:
391 |           Node child = n.getFirstChild();
392 |           String name = null;
393 |           // NOTE: The normalization pass insures that local names do not
394 |           // collide with global names.
395 |           if (child.isName()) {
396 |             name = child.getString();
397 |           } else if (child.isFunction()) {
398 |             name = anonFunctionMap.get(child);
399 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
400 |             Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isGet(child));
401 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = child.getFirstChild();
402 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.isName()) {
403 |               name = fnIdentifingNode.getString();
404 |             } else if (fnIdentifingNode.isFunction()) {
405 |               name = anonFunctionMap.get(fnIdentifingNode);
406 |             }
407 |           }
408 | 
409 |           if (name != null) {
410 |             FunctionState fs = functionMap.get(name);
411 |             // Only visit call-sites for functions that can be inlined.
412 |             if (fs != null) {
413 |               callback.visitCallSite(t, n, parent, fs);
414 |             }
415 |           }
416 |           break;
417 |       }
418 |     }

   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,

503 |   private static boolean mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node n) {
504 |     switch (n.getType()) {

514 |       case Token.CONTINUE:

517 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
518 |       case Token.FOR:
519 |       case Token.IF:
520 |       case Token.LABEL:
521 |       case Token.SWITCH:
522 |       case Token.THROW:
523 |       case Token.TRY:
524 |       case Token.VAR:
525 |       case Token.WHILE:
526 |       case Token.WITH:
527 |         return true;
528 | 
529 |       default:
530 |         return false;
531 |     }

Original range: 120-285

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.opToStr(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2178-2223
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
2178 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2179 |     switch (operator) {
2180 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2181 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2182 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2183 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
2184 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
2185 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
2186 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2187 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2188 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2189 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2190 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
2191 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
2192 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2193 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2194 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2195 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
2196 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
2197 |       case Token.GT: return ">";

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0158 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 791-807
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
791 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
792 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
793 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
794 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
795 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
796 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
797 |     }
798 | 
799 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
800 | 
801 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
802 |     do {
803 |       current = current.getParent();
804 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
805 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
806 |     } while (current != n);
807 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 180-205
Documentation:

Method code:
180 |   void add(String newcode) {
181 |     maybeEndStatement();
182 | 
183 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
184 |       return;
185 |     }
186 | 
187 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
188 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
189 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
190 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
191 |       // For example: "return foo;"
192 |       append(" ");
193 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
194 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
195 |       // For example,
196 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
197 |       // is valid and should print like
198 |       // / // / /
199 |       append(" ");
200 |     } else if (c == '"' && isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
201 |       maybeInsertSpace();
202 |     }
203 | 
204 |     append(newcode);
205 |   }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1827-1880
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1827 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1828 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1829 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1830 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1831 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1832 |       } else {
1833 |         // Otherwise, only its children can be removed.
1834 |         node.detachChildren();
1835 |       }
1836 |     } else if (node.isCatch()) {
1837 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1838 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1839 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1840 |       node.detachFromParent();
1841 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1842 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1843 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1844 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1845 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1846 |       node.detachChildren();
1847 |     } else if (node.isBlock()) {
1848 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1849 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1850 |       node.detachChildren();
1851 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1852 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1853 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1854 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1855 |     } else if (parent.isVar()) {
1856 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1857 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1858 |       } else {
1859 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1860 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1861 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1862 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1863 |       }
1864 |     } else if (parent.isLabel()
1865 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1866 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1867 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1868 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1869 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1870 |     } else if (parent.isFor()
1871 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1872 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1873 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1874 |       // or the structure removed.
1875 |       parent.replaceChild(node, IR.empty());
1876 |     } else {
1877 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1878 |           node.toString() + " of " + parent.toString());
1879 |     }
1880 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.addGuard(WarningsGuard)

Method code:
86 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
87 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
88 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
89 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
90 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
94 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
95 |     } else {
96 |       numberOfAdds++;
97 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
98 |       guards.remove(guard);
99 |       guards.add(guard);
100 |     }
101 |   }

===== M0162 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.trimCanidatesUsingOnCost()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 663-680
Documentation:
/**
   * Remove entries from the list of candidates that can't be inlined.
   */
Method code:
663 |   void trimCanidatesUsingOnCost() {
664 |     Iterator<Entry<String, FunctionState>> i;
665 |     for (i = fns.entrySet().iterator(); i.hasNext();) {
666 |       FunctionState fs = i.next().getValue();
667 |       if (fs.hasReferences()) {
668 |         // Only inline function if it decreases the code size.
669 |         boolean lowersCost = mimimizeCost(fs);
670 |         if (!lowersCost) {
671 |           // It shouldn't be inlined; remove it from the list.
672 |           i.remove();
673 |         }
674 |       } else if (!fs.canRemove()) {
675 |         // Don't bother tracking functions without references that can't be
676 |         // removed.
677 |         i.remove();
678 |       }
679 |     }
680 |   }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.exposeExpression(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 146-235
Documentation:
/**
   * Rewrite the expression such that the sub-expression is in a movable
   * expression statement while maintaining evaluation order.
   *
   * Two types of subexpressions are extracted from the source expression:
   * 1) subexpressions with side-effects.
   * 2) conditional expressions, that contain the call, which are transformed
   * into IF statements.
   *
   * The following terms are used:
   *    expressionRoot: The top-level node before which the any extracted
   *                    expressions should be placed before.
   *    nonconditionalExpr: The node that will be extracted either expres.
   *
   */
Method code:
146 |   private void exposeExpression(Node expressionRoot, Node subExpression) {
147 |     Node nonconditionalExpr = findNonconditionalParent(
148 |         subExpression, expressionRoot);
149 |     // Before extraction, record whether there are side-effect
150 |     boolean hasFollowingSideEffects = NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(
151 |         nonconditionalExpr, compiler);
152 | 
153 |     Node exprInjectionPoint = findInjectionPoint(nonconditionalExpr);
154 |     DecompositionState state = new DecompositionState();
155 |     state.sideEffects = hasFollowingSideEffects;
156 |     state.extractBeforeStatement = exprInjectionPoint;
157 | 
158 |     // Extract expressions in the reverse order of their evaluation.
159 |     for (Node grandchild = null,
160 |             child = nonconditionalExpr,
161 |             parent = child.getParent();
162 |          parent != expressionRoot;
163 |          grandchild = child,
164 |              child = parent,
165 |              parent = child.getParent()) {
166 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
167 |       Preconditions.checkState(
168 |           !isConditionalOp(parent) || child == parent.getFirstChild());
169 |       if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
170 |           if (isSafeAssign(parent, state.sideEffects)) {
171 |             // It is always safe to inline "foo()" for expressions such as
172 |             // "a = b = c = foo();"
173 |             // As the assignment is unaffected by side effect of "foo()"
174 |             // and the names assigned-to can not influence the state before
175 |             // the call to foo.
176 |             //
177 |             // This is not true of more complex LHS values, such as
178 |             // a.x = foo();
179 |             // next().x = foo();
180 |             // in these cases the checks below are necessary.
181 |           } else {
182 |             // Alias "next()" in "next().foo"
183 |             Node left = parent.getFirstChild();
184 |             int type = left.getType();
185 |             if (left != child) {
186 |               Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isGet(left));
187 |               if (type == Token.GETELEM) {
188 |                 decomposeSubExpressions(left.getLastChild(), null, state);
189 |               }
190 |               decomposeSubExpressions(left.getFirstChild(), null, state);
191 |             }
192 |           }
193 |       } else if (parentType == Token.CALL
194 |           && NodeUtil.isGet(parent.getFirstChild())) {
195 |         Node functionExpression = parent.getFirstChild();
196 |         decomposeSubExpressions(functionExpression.getNext(), child, state);
197 |         // Now handle the call expression
198 |         if (isExpressionTreeUnsafe(functionExpression, state.sideEffects)
199 |             && functionExpression.getFirstChild() != grandchild) {
200 |           // TODO(johnlenz): In Internet Explorer, non-JavaScript objects such
201 |           // as DOM objects can not be decomposed.
202 |           Preconditions.checkState(allowObjectCallDecomposing(),
203 |               "Object method calls can not be decomposed.");
204 |           // Either there were preexisting side-effects, or this node has
205 |           // side-effects.
206 |           state.sideEffects = true;
207 | 
208 |           // Rewrite the call so "this" is preserved.
209 |           Node replacement = rewriteCallExpression(parent, state);
210 |           // Continue from here.
211 |           parent = replacement;
212 |         }
213 |       } else if (parentType == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
214 |         decomposeObjectLiteralKeys(parent.getFirstChild(), child, state);
215 |       } else {
216 |         decomposeSubExpressions(parent.getFirstChild(), child, state);
217 |       }
218 |     }
219 | 
220 |     // Now extract the expression that the decomposition is being performed to
221 |     // to allow to be moved.  All expressions that need to be evaluated before
222 |     // this have been extracted, so add the expression statement after the
223 |     // other extracted expressions and the original statement (or replace
224 |     // the original statement.
225 |     if (nonconditionalExpr == subExpression) {
226 |       // Don't extract the call, as that introduces an extra constant VAR
227 |       // that will simply need to be inlined back.  It will be handled as
228 |       // an EXPRESSION call site type.
229 |       // Node extractedCall = extractExpression(decomposition, expressionRoot);
230 |     } else {
231 |       Node parent = nonconditionalExpr.getParent();
232 |       boolean needResult = !parent.isExprResult();
233 |       extractConditional(nonconditionalExpr, exprInjectionPoint, needResult);
234 |     }
235 |   }

   * A post-order traversal, calling Visitor.visit for each child matching

Method code:
2805 |   static void visitPostOrder(Node node,
2806 |                      Visitor visitor,
2807 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2808 |     if (traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2809 |       for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2810 |         visitPostOrder(c, visitor, traverseChildrenPred);
2811 |       }
2812 |     }
2813 | 
2814 |     visitor.visit(node);
2815 |   }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java

Method code:
107 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
108 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
109 |       return;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     int type = n.getType();
113 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
114 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
115 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
116 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
117 | 
118 |     // Handle all binary operators
119 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
120 |       Preconditions.checkState(
121 |           childCount == 2,
122 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
123 |           opstr, childCount);
124 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
125 | 
126 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
127 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
128 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
129 | 
130 |       if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
131 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
132 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
133 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
134 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
135 |       } else {
136 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
137 |       }
138 |       return;
139 |     }
140 | 
141 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
142 | 
143 |     switch (type) {
144 |       case Token.TRY: {
145 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
146 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
147 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
148 | 
149 |         add("try");
150 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
151 | 
152 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
153 |         // isn't a catch block
154 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
155 |         if (catchblock != null) {
156 |           add(catchblock);
157 |         }
158 | 
159 |         if (childCount == 3) {
160 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
161 |           add("finally");
162 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
163 |         }
164 |         break;
165 |       }
166 | 
167 |       case Token.CATCH:
168 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
169 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
170 |         add("catch");
171 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
172 |         add("(");
173 |         add(first);
174 |         add(")");
175 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.THROW:
179 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
180 |         add("throw");
181 |         add(first);
182 | 
183 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
184 |         // parse this.
185 |         cc.endStatement(true);
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.RETURN:
189 |         add("return");
190 |         if (childCount == 1) {
191 |           add(first);
192 |         } else {
193 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
194 |         }
195 |         cc.endStatement();
196 |         break;
197 | 
198 |       case Token.VAR:
199 |         if (first != null) {
200 |           add("var ");
201 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 | 
205 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
206 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
207 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.NAME:
211 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
212 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
213 |         } else {
214 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
215 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
216 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
217 |           if (first.isComma()) {
218 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
219 |           } else {
220 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
221 |             // precedence.
222 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
228 |         add("[");
229 |         addArrayList(first);
230 |         add("]");
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
234 |         add("(");
235 |         addList(first);
236 |         add(")");
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.COMMA:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
241 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context,
242 |             getContextForNoInOperator(context), 0, 0);
243 |         break;
244 | 
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
247 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
248 |         break;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
251 |       case Token.VOID:
252 |       case Token.NOT:
253 |       case Token.BITNOT:
254 |       case Token.POS: {
255 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
256 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
257 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
258 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
259 |         break;
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       case Token.NEG: {
263 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
264 | 
265 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
266 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
267 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
268 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
269 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
270 |         } else {
271 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
272 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         break;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       case Token.HOOK: {
279 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
280 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
281 |         Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
282 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
283 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
284 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, rhsContext);
285 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
286 |         addExpr(last, 1, rhsContext);
287 |         break;
288 |       }
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REGEXP:
291 |         if (!first.isString() ||
292 |             !last.isString()) {
293 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
294 |         }
295 | 
296 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
297 | 
298 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
299 |         if (childCount == 2) {
300 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
301 |         } else {
302 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
303 |           add(regexp);
304 |         }
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
308 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
309 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
310 |         }
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
312 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
313 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
314 |           add("(");
315 |         }
316 | 
317 |         add("function");
318 |         add(first);
319 | 
320 |         add(first.getNext());
321 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
322 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
323 | 
324 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
325 |           add(")");
326 |         }
327 |         break;
328 | 
329 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
330 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
331 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
332 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
333 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
334 | 
335 |         // Get methods are unnamed
336 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
337 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
338 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
339 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
340 |           add("get ");
341 |         } else {
342 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
343 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
344 |           add("set ");
345 |         }
346 | 
347 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
348 |         String name = n.getString();
349 |         Node fn = first;
350 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
351 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
352 | 
353 |         // Add the property name.
354 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
355 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
356 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
357 |             // Unicode escaped.
358 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
359 |           add(name);
360 |         } else {
361 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
362 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
363 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
364 |             cc.addNumber(d);
365 |           } else {
366 |             addJsString(n);
367 |           }
368 |         }
369 | 
370 |         add(parameters);
371 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
372 |         break;
373 | 
374 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
375 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
376 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
377 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
378 |         }
379 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
380 |         if (preserveBlock) {
381 |           cc.beginBlock();
382 |         }
383 | 
384 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
385 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
386 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
387 |                 !preserveBlock &&
388 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
389 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
390 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
391 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
392 | 
393 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
394 |           if (c.isVar()) {
395 |             cc.endStatement();
396 |           }
397 | 
398 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
399 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
400 |           }
401 | 
402 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
403 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
404 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
405 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
406 |           }
407 |         }
408 |         if (preserveBlock) {
409 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
410 |         }
411 |         break;
412 |       }
413 | 
414 |       case Token.FOR:
415 |         if (childCount == 4) {
416 |           add("for");
417 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
418 |           add("(");
419 |           if (first.isVar()) {
420 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
421 |           } else {
422 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
423 |           }
424 |           add(";");
425 |           add(first.getNext());
426 |           add(";");
427 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
428 |           add(")");
429 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
430 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
431 |         } else {
432 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
433 |           add("for");
434 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
435 |           add("(");
436 |           add(first);
437 |           add("in");
438 |           add(first.getNext());
439 |           add(")");
440 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
441 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
442 |         }
443 |         break;
444 | 
445 |       case Token.DO:
446 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
447 |         add("do");
448 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
449 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
450 |         add("while");
451 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
452 |         add("(");
453 |         add(last);
454 |         add(")");
455 |         cc.endStatement();
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.WHILE:
459 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
460 |         add("while");
461 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
462 |         add("(");
463 |         add(first);
464 |         add(")");
465 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
466 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
467 |         break;
468 | 
469 |       case Token.EMPTY:
470 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
474 |         Preconditions.checkState(
475 |             childCount == 2,
476 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
477 |         Preconditions.checkState(
478 |             last.isString(),
479 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
480 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
481 |         if (needsParens) {
482 |           add("(");
483 |         }
484 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
485 |         if (needsParens) {
486 |           add(")");
487 |         }
488 |         if (this.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3
489 |             && TokenStream.isKeyword(last.getString())) {
490 |           // Check for ECMASCRIPT3 keywords.
491 |           add("[");
492 |           add(last);
493 |           add("]");
494 |         } else {
495 |           add(".");
496 |           addIdentifier(last.getString());
497 |         }
498 |         break;
499 |       }
500 | 
501 |       case Token.GETELEM:
502 |         Preconditions.checkState(
503 |             childCount == 2,
504 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
505 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
506 |         add("[");
507 |         add(first.getNext());
508 |         add("]");
509 |         break;
510 | 
511 |       case Token.WITH:
512 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
513 |         add("with(");
514 |         add(first);
515 |         add(")");
516 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
517 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
518 |         break;
519 | 
520 |       case Token.INC:
521 |       case Token.DEC: {
522 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
523 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
524 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
525 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
526 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
527 |         if (postProp != 0) {
528 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
529 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
530 |         } else {
531 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
532 |           add(first);
533 |         }
534 |         break;
535 |       }
536 | 
537 |       case Token.CALL:
538 |         // We have two special cases here:
539 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
540 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
541 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
542 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
543 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
544 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
545 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
546 |         // that must be preserved.
547 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
548 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
549 |           add("(0,");
550 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
551 |           add(")");
552 |         } else {
553 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
554 |         }
555 |         add("(");
556 |         addList(first.getNext());
557 |         add(")");
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.IF:
561 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
562 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
563 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
564 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
565 |           cc.beginBlock();
566 |         }
567 | 
568 |         add("if");
569 |         cc.maybeInsertSpace();
570 |         add("(");
571 |         add(first);
572 |         add(")");
573 | 
574 |         if (hasElse) {
575 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
576 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
577 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
578 |           add("else");
579 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
580 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
581 |         } else {
582 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
583 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
584 |         }
585 | 
586 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
587 |           cc.endBlock();
588 |         }
589 |         break;
590 | 
591 |       case Token.NULL:
592 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
593 |         cc.addConstant("null");
594 |         break;
595 | 
596 |       case Token.THIS:
597 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
598 |         add("this");
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.FALSE:
602 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
603 |         cc.addConstant("false");
604 |         break;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.TRUE:
607 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
608 |         cc.addConstant("true");
609 |         break;
610 | 
611 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
612 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
613 |         add("continue");
614 |         if (childCount == 1) {
615 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
616 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
617 |           }
618 |           add(" ");
619 |           add(first);
620 |         }
621 |         cc.endStatement();
622 |         break;
623 | 
624 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
625 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
626 |         add("debugger");
627 |         cc.endStatement();
628 |         break;
629 | 
630 |       case Token.BREAK:
631 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
632 |         add("break");
633 |         if (childCount == 1) {
634 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
635 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
636 |           }
637 |           add(" ");
638 |           add(first);
639 |         }
640 |         cc.endStatement();
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
646 |         cc.endStatement();
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.NEW:
650 |         add("new ");
651 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
652 | 
653 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
654 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
655 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
656 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
657 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
658 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
659 |         }
660 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
661 | 
662 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
663 |         Node next = first.getNext();
664 |         if (next != null) {
665 |           add("(");
666 |           addList(next);
667 |           add(")");
668 |         }
669 |         break;
670 | 
671 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
672 |         Preconditions.checkState(
673 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
674 |         addJsString(n);
675 |         break;
676 | 
677 |       case Token.STRING:
678 |         Preconditions.checkState(
679 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
680 |         addJsString(n);
681 |         break;
682 | 
683 |       case Token.DELPROP:
684 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
685 |         add("delete ");
686 |         add(first);
687 |         break;
688 | 
689 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
690 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
691 |         if (needsParens) {
692 |           add("(");
693 |         }
694 |         add("{");
695 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
696 |           if (c != first) {
697 |             cc.listSeparator();
698 |           }
699 | 
700 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
701 |             add(c);
702 |           } else {
703 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
704 |             String key = c.getString();
705 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
706 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
707 |             if (!c.isQuotedString()
708 |                 && !(languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3
709 |                     && TokenStream.isKeyword(key))
710 |                 && TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key)
711 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
712 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
713 |                 && NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
714 |               add(key);
715 |             } else {
716 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
717 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
718 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
719 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
720 |               } else {
721 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
722 |               }
723 |             }
724 |             add(":");
725 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
726 |           }
727 |         }
728 |         add("}");
729 |         if (needsParens) {
730 |           add(")");
731 |         }
732 |         break;
733 |       }
734 | 
735 |       case Token.SWITCH:
736 |         add("switch(");
737 |         add(first);
738 |         add(")");
739 |         cc.beginBlock();
740 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
741 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
742 |         break;
743 | 
744 |       case Token.CASE:
745 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
746 |         add("case ");
747 |         add(first);
748 |         addCaseBody(last);
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
752 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
753 |         add("default");
754 |         addCaseBody(first);
755 |         break;
756 | 
757 |       case Token.LABEL:
758 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
759 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
760 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
761 |         }
762 |         add(first);
763 |         add(":");
764 |         if (!last.isBlock()) {
765 |           cc.maybeInsertSpace();
766 |         }
767 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
768 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
769 |         break;
770 | 
771 |       case Token.CAST:
772 |         add("(");
773 |         add(first);
774 |         add(")");
775 |         break;
776 | 
777 |       default:
778 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
779 |     }
780 | 
781 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
782 |   }

173 |     @Override
174 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
175 |       if (!node.isCall() && !node.isNew()) {
176 |         return;
177 |       }
178 | 
179 |       Collection<Definition> definitions =
180 |           defFinder.getDefinitionsReferencedAt(node.getFirstChild());
181 |       if (definitions == null) {
182 |         return;

188 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(lValue);

190 |           maybeFunction = true;
191 |           if (!noSideEffectFunctionNames.contains(lValue)) {
192 |             return;
193 |           }
194 |         }
195 |       }
196 | 
197 |       if (maybeFunction) {
198 |         node.setSideEffectFlags(Node.NO_SIDE_EFFECTS);
199 |       }

===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(AbstractCompiler,Node,Node,Map<String, Node>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 61-107
Documentation:

Method code:
61 |   static Node inject(AbstractCompiler compiler, Node node, Node parent,
62 |       Map<String, Node> replacements, boolean replaceThis) {
63 |     if (node.isName()) {
64 |       Node replacementTemplate = replacements.get(node.getString());
65 |       if (replacementTemplate != null) {
66 |         // This should not be replacing declared names.
67 |         Preconditions.checkState(!parent.isFunction()
68 |             || !parent.isVar()
69 |             || !parent.isCatch());
70 |         // The name may need to be replaced more than once,
71 |         // so we need to clone the node.
72 |         Node replacement = replacementTemplate.cloneTree();
73 |         parent.replaceChild(node, replacement);
74 |         return replacement;
75 |       }
76 |     } else if (replaceThis && node.isThis()) {
77 |       Node replacementTemplate = replacements.get(THIS_MARKER);
78 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(replacementTemplate);
79 |       if (!replacementTemplate.isThis()) {
80 |         // The name may need to be replaced more than once,
81 |         // so we need to clone the node.
82 |         Node replacement = replacementTemplate.cloneTree();
83 |         parent.replaceChild(node, replacement);
84 | 
85 |         // Remove the value.  This isn't required but it ensures that we won't
86 |         // inject side-effects multiple times as it will trigger the null
87 |         // check above if we do.
88 |         if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(replacementTemplate, compiler)) {
89 |           replacements.remove(THIS_MARKER);
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         return replacement;
93 |       }
94 |     } else if (node.isFunction()) {
95 |       // Once we enter another scope the "this" value changes, don't try
96 |       // to replace it within an inner scope.
97 |       replaceThis = false;
98 |     }
99 | 
100 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
101 |       // We have to reassign c in case it was replaced, because the removed c's
102 |       // getNext() would no longer be correct.
103 |       c = inject(compiler, c, node, replacements, replaceThis);
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     return node;
107 |   }

===== M0223 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-624
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
609 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
610 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
611 |       Set<String> directives = null;
612 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
613 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
614 |         if (directives == null) {
615 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
616 |         } else {
617 |           directives.add(directive);
618 |         }
619 |       }
620 | 
621 |       if (directives != null) {
622 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
623 |       }
624 |     }

660 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
661 |       CompilerOptions options, JSTypeRegistry registry,

663 |     Preconditions.checkState(options.sourceMapDetailLevel != null);

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getPrototype()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 366-388
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */
Method code:
366 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
367 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field
368 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {
369 |       String refName = getReferenceName();
370 |       if (refName == null) {
371 |         // Someone is trying to access the prototype of a structural function.
372 |         // We don't want to give real properties to this prototype, because
373 |         // then it would propagate to all structural functions.
374 |         setPrototypeNoCheck(
375 |            registry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE),
376 |            null);
377 |       } else {
378 |         setPrototype(
379 |             new PrototypeObjectType(
380 |                 registry,
381 |                 getReferenceName() + ".prototype",
382 |                 registry.getNativeObjectType(OBJECT_TYPE),
383 |                 isNativeObjectType(), null),
384 |             null);
385 |       }
386 |     }
387 |     return (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
388 |   }

===== M0245 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2482-2491
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2482 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2483 |     int len = s.length();
2484 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2485 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2486 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2487 |         return false;
2488 |       }
2489 |     }
2490 |     return true;
2491 |   }
