===== M0252 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 790-935
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
790 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
791 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
792 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
793 |     // that we know to be safe
794 |     switch (n.getType()) {
795 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
796 |       case Token.CAST:
797 |       case Token.AND:
798 |       case Token.BLOCK:
799 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
800 |       case Token.HOOK:
801 |       case Token.IF:
802 |       case Token.IN:
803 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
804 |       case Token.NUMBER:
805 |       case Token.OR:
806 |       case Token.THIS:
807 |       case Token.TRUE:
808 |       case Token.FALSE:
809 |       case Token.NULL:
810 |       case Token.STRING:
811 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
812 |       case Token.SWITCH:
813 |       case Token.TRY:
814 |       case Token.EMPTY:
815 |         break;
816 | 
817 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
818 |       case Token.THROW:
819 |         return true;
820 | 
821 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
822 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
823 |           return true;
824 |         }
825 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
826 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
827 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
828 |             return true;
829 |           }
830 |         }
831 |         return false;
832 | 
833 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
834 |       case Token.REGEXP:
835 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
836 |           return true;
837 |         }
838 |         break;
839 | 
840 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
841 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
842 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
843 |           return true;
844 |         }
845 |         break;
846 | 
847 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
848 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
849 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
850 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
851 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
852 | 
853 |       case Token.NEW:
854 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
855 |           return true;
856 |         }
857 | 
858 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
859 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
860 |           // side-effects
861 |           break;
862 |         }
863 |         return true;
864 | 
865 |       case Token.CALL:
866 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
867 |         // side effect property set.
868 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
869 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
870 |           // side-effects
871 |           break;
872 |         }
873 |         return true;
874 | 
875 |       default:
876 |         if (isSimpleOperator(n)) {
877 |           break;
878 |         }
879 | 
880 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
881 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
882 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
883 |             return true;
884 |           }
885 | 
886 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
887 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
888 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
889 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
890 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
891 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
892 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
893 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
894 |             return true;
895 |           }
896 | 
897 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
898 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
899 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
900 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
901 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
902 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
903 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
904 |               return false;
905 |             }
906 | 
907 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
908 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
909 |             // other objects.
910 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
911 |             // side-effect.
912 |             while (isGet(current)) {
913 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
914 |             }
915 | 
916 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
917 |           } else {
918 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
919 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
920 |             // preserve existing behavior.
921 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
922 |           }
923 |         }
924 | 
925 |         return true;
926 |     }
927 | 
928 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
929 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
930 |         return true;
931 |       }
932 |     }
933 | 
934 |     return false;
935 |   }

===== M0281 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MarkNoSideEffectCalls.definitionTypeContainsFunctionType(Definition)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MarkNoSideEffectCalls.java
Original range: 77-98
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the type of the value of the RHS expression can
   * be a function node.
   */
Method code:
77 |   private static boolean definitionTypeContainsFunctionType(Definition def) {
78 |     Node rhs = def.getRValue();
79 |     if (rhs == null) {
80 |       return true;
81 |     }
82 | 
83 |     switch (rhs.getType()) {
84 |       case Token.ASSIGN:

   * Add all children after 'node'.

Method code:
667 |   public void addChildrenAfter(Node children, Node node) {
668 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node == null || node.parent == this);
669 |     for (Node child = children; child != null; child = child.next) {
670 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(child.parent == null);

685 |         lastSibling.next = first;
686 |       } else {
687 |         last = lastSibling;
688 |       }
689 |       first = children;
690 |     }
691 |   }

===== M0310 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 588-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured variables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
588 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
589 |     switch (n.getType()) {
590 |       case Token.CAST:
591 |         return isLiteralValue(n.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions);
592 | 
593 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
594 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
595 |              child = child.getNext()) {
596 |           if ((!child.isEmpty()) && !isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
597 |             return false;
598 |           }
599 |         }
600 |         return true;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.REGEXP:
603 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
604 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
605 |              child = child.getNext()) {
606 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
607 |             return false;
608 |           }
609 |         }
610 |         return true;
611 | 
612 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
613 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
614 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
615 |              child = child.getNext()) {
616 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
617 |             return false;
618 |           }
619 |         }
620 |         return true;
621 | 
622 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
623 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
624 | 
625 |       default:
626 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
627 |     }
628 |   }

===== M0312 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1193-1201
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1193 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1194 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1195 |       return -1;
1196 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1197 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1198 |     } else {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1200 |     }
1201 |   }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 72-107
Documentation:

Method code:
72 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
73 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
74 |       if (inputId == null) {
75 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
76 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
77 |         // from the AST.
78 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
79 |       }
80 | 
81 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
82 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
83 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
84 | 
85 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
86 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
87 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
88 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
89 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
90 |       }
91 | 
92 |       // Args: Declare function variables
93 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
94 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
95 |            a = a.getNext()) {
96 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
97 |         declareVar(a);
98 |       }
99 | 
100 |       // Body
101 |       scanVars(body);
102 |     } else {
103 |       // It's the global block
104 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
105 |       scanVars(n);
106 |     }
107 |   }

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2965-3031
Documentation:
/**
   * @param locals A predicate to apply to unknown local values.
   * @return Whether the node is known to be a value that is not a reference
   *     outside the expression scope.
   */
Method code:
2965 |   static boolean evaluatesToLocalValue(Node value, Predicate<Node> locals) {
2966 |     switch (value.getType()) {
2967 |       case Token.CAST:
2968 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals);
2969 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
2970 |         // A result that is aliased by a non-local name, is the effectively the
2971 |         // same as returning a non-local name, but this doesn't matter if the
2972 |         // value is immutable.
2973 |         return NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value.getLastChild())
2974 |             || (locals.apply(value)
2975 |                 && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals));
2976 |       case Token.COMMA:
2977 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2978 |       case Token.AND:
2979 |       case Token.OR:
2980 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals)
2981 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2982 |       case Token.HOOK:
2983 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild().getNext(), locals)
2984 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2985 |       case Token.INC:
2986 |       case Token.DEC:
2987 |         if (value.getBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP)) {
2988 |           return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals);
2989 |         } else {
2990 |           return true;
2991 |         }
2992 |       case Token.THIS:
2993 |         return locals.apply(value);
2994 |       case Token.NAME:
2995 |         return isImmutableValue(value) || locals.apply(value);
2996 |       case Token.GETELEM:
2997 |       case Token.GETPROP:
2998 |         // There is no information about the locality of object properties.
2999 |         return locals.apply(value);
3000 |       case Token.CALL:
3001 |         return callHasLocalResult(value)
3002 |             || isToStringMethodCall(value)
3003 |             || locals.apply(value);
3004 |       case Token.NEW:
3005 |         return newHasLocalResult(value)
3006 |                || locals.apply(value);
3007 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
3008 |       case Token.REGEXP:
3009 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
3010 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
3011 |         // Literals objects with non-literal children are allowed.
3012 |         return true;
3013 |       case Token.DELPROP:
3014 |       case Token.IN:
3015 |         // TODO(johnlenz): should IN operator be included in #isSimpleOperator?
3016 |         return true;
3017 |       default:
3018 |         // Other op force a local value:
3019 |         //  x = '' + g (x is now an local string)
3020 |         //  x -= g (x is now an local number)
3021 |         if (isAssignmentOp(value)
3022 |             || isSimpleOperator(value)
3023 |             || isImmutableValue(value)) {
3024 |           return true;
3025 |         }
3026 | 
3027 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
3028 |             "Unexpected expression node" + value +
3029 |             "\n parent:" + value.getParent());
3030 |     }
3031 |   }

===== M0331 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineCostDelta(Node,Set<String>,InliningMode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 884-935
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The difference between the function definition cost and
   *     inline cost.
   */
Method code:
884 |   private static int inlineCostDelta(
885 |       Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias, InliningMode mode) {
886 |     // The part of the function that is never inlined:
887 |     //    "function xx(xx,xx){}" (15 + (param count * 3) -1;
888 |     int paramCount = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).getChildCount();
889 |     int commaCount = (paramCount > 1) ? paramCount - 1 : 0;
890 |     int costDeltaFunctionOverhead = 15 + commaCount +
891 |         (paramCount * InlineCostEstimator.ESTIMATED_IDENTIFIER_COST);
892 | 
893 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
894 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
895 |       // Assume the inline cost is zero for empty functions.
896 |       return -costDeltaFunctionOverhead;
897 |     }
898 | 
899 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
900 |       // The part of the function that is inlined using direct inlining:
901 |       //    "return " (7)
902 |       return -(costDeltaFunctionOverhead + 7);
903 |     } else {
904 |       int aliasCount = namesToAlias.size();
905 | 
906 |       // Originally, we estimated purely base on the function code size, relying
907 |       // on later optimizations. But that did not produce good results, so here
908 |       // we try to estimate the something closer to the actual inlined coded.
909 | 
910 |       // NOTE 1: Result overhead is only if there is an assignment, but
911 |       // getting that information would require some refactoring.
912 |       // NOTE 2: The aliasing overhead is currently an under-estimate,
913 |       // as some parameters are aliased because of the parameters used.
914 |       // Perhaps we should just assume all parameters will be aliased?
915 |       final int inlineBlockOverhead = 4; // "X:{}"
916 |       final int perReturnOverhead = 2;   // "return" --> "break X"
917 |       final int perReturnResultOverhead = 3; // "XX="
918 |       final int perAliasOverhead = 3; // "XX="
919 | 
920 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Counting the number of returns is relatively expensive
921 |       //   this information should be determined during the traversal and
922 |       //   cached.
923 |       int returnCount = NodeUtil.getNodeTypeReferenceCount(
924 |           block, Token.RETURN, new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
925 |       int resultCount = (returnCount > 0) ? returnCount - 1 : 0;
926 |       int baseOverhead = (returnCount > 0) ? inlineBlockOverhead : 0;
927 | 
928 |       int overhead = baseOverhead
929 |           + returnCount * perReturnOverhead
930 |           + resultCount * perReturnResultOverhead
931 |           + aliasCount * perAliasOverhead;
932 | 
933 |       return (overhead - costDeltaFunctionOverhead);
934 |     }
935 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(Node,Node,Supplier<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 112-149
Documentation:
/**
   * Get a mapping for function parameter names to call arguments.
   */
Method code:
112 |   static LinkedHashMap<String, Node> getFunctionCallParameterMap(
113 |       Node fnNode, Node callNode, Supplier<String> safeNameIdSupplier) {
114 |     // Create an argName -> expression map
115 |     // NOTE: A linked map is created here to provide ordering.
116 |     LinkedHashMap<String, Node> argMap = Maps.newLinkedHashMap();
117 | 
118 |     // CALL NODE: [ NAME, ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
119 |     Node cArg = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
120 |     if (cArg != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
121 |       argMap.put(THIS_MARKER, cArg);
122 |       cArg = cArg.getNext();
123 |     } else {
124 |       // 'apply' isn't supported yet.
125 |       Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode));
126 |       argMap.put(THIS_MARKER, NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(callNode));
127 |     }
128 | 
129 |     for (Node fnArg : NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).children()) {
130 |       if (cArg != null) {
131 |         argMap.put(fnArg.getString(), cArg);
132 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
133 |       } else {
134 |         Node srcLocation = callNode;
135 |         argMap.put(fnArg.getString(), NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation));
136 |       }
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     // Add temp names for arguments that don't have named parameters in the
140 |     // called function.
141 |     while (cArg != null) {
142 |       String uniquePlaceholder =
143 |         getUniqueAnonymousParameterName(safeNameIdSupplier);
144 |       argMap.put(uniquePlaceholder, cArg);
145 |       cArg = cArg.getNext();
146 |     }
147 | 
148 |     return argMap;
149 |   }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.canExposeExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 712-718
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine whether a expression is movable, or can be be made movable be
   * decomposing the containing expression.
   *
   * An subExpression is MOVABLE if it can be replaced with a temporary holding
   * its results and moved to immediately before the root of the expression.
   * There are three conditions that must be met for this to occur:
   * 1) There must be a location to inject a statement for the expression.  For
   * example, this condition can not be met if the expression is a loop
   * condition or CASE condition.
   * 2) If the expression can be affect by side-effects, there can not be a
   * side-effect between original location and the expression root.
   * 3) If the expression has side-effects, there can not be any other
   * expression that can be effected between the original location and the
   * expression root.
   *
   * An expression is DECOMPOSABLE if it can be rewritten so that an
   * subExpression is MOVABLE.
   *
   * An expression is decomposed by moving any other sub-expressions that
   * preventing an subExpression from being MOVABLE.
   *
   * @return Whether This is a call that can be moved to an new point in the
   * AST to allow it to be inlined.
   */
Method code:
712 |   DecompositionType canExposeExpression(Node subExpression) {
713 |     Node expressionRoot = findExpressionRoot(subExpression);
714 |     if (expressionRoot != null) {
715 |       return isSubexpressionMovable(expressionRoot, subExpression);
716 |     }
717 |     return DecompositionType.UNDECOMPOSABLE;
718 |   }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

1207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:

1210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1213 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1214 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1215 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1216 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1217 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1218 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1219 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1220 |       case Token.EQ:
1221 |       case Token.NE:
1222 |       case Token.SHEQ:

1224 |       case Token.LT:

1226 |       case Token.LE:
1227 |       case Token.GE:
1228 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1229 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1230 |       case Token.LSH:
1231 |       case Token.RSH:
1232 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1233 |       case Token.SUB:
1234 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1235 |       case Token.MUL:
1236 |       case Token.MOD:
1237 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1238 |       case Token.INC:
1239 |       case Token.DEC:
1240 |       case Token.NEW:
1241 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1242 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1243 |       case Token.VOID:
1244 |       case Token.NOT:
1245 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1246 |       case Token.POS:
1247 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1248 | 
1249 |       case Token.CALL:
1250 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1251 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1252 |       // Data values
1253 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1254 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.

===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1240-1300
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1240 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1241 |     // unknown
1242 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1243 |       return true;
1244 |     }
1245 |     // all type
1246 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1247 |       return true;
1248 |     }
1249 |     // equality
1250 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1251 |       return true;
1252 |     }
1253 |     // unions
1254 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1255 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1256 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1257 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1258 |           return true;
1259 |         }
1260 |       }
1261 |       return false;
1262 |     }
1263 | 
1264 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1265 |     // are not equivalent.
1266 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1267 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1268 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1269 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1270 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1271 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1272 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1273 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1274 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1275 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1276 | 
1277 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1278 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1279 |     } else {
1280 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1281 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1282 |     }
1283 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1284 |       return false;
1285 |     }
1286 | 
1287 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1288 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1289 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1290 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1291 |               thatType);
1292 |     }
1293 | 
1294 |     // proxy types
1295 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1296 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1297 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1298 |     }
1299 |     return false;
1300 |   }

===== M0389 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.ReferencedAfterSideEffect.visit(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 395-428
Documentation:

Method code:
395 |     @Override
396 |     public void visit(Node n) {
397 |       // If we are exiting a loop.
398 |       if (NodeUtil.isLoopStructure(n)) {
399 |         loopsEntered--;
400 |         if (!inLoop() && !sideEffectSeen) {
401 |           // Now that the loops has been fully traversed and
402 |           // no side-effects have been seen, throw away
403 |           // the references seen in them.
404 |           parametersReferenced.clear();
405 |         }
406 |       }
407 | 
408 |       if (!sideEffectSeen) {
409 |         // Look for side-effects.
410 |         if (hasNonLocalSideEffect(n)) {
411 |           sideEffectSeen = true;
412 |         }
413 |       }
414 | 
415 |       // If traversing the nodes of a loop save any references
416 |       // that are seen.
417 |       if (inLoop() || sideEffectSeen) {
418 |         // Record references to parameters.
419 |         if (n.isName()) {
420 |           String name = n.getString();
421 |           if (parameters.contains(name)) {
422 |             parametersReferenced.add(name);
423 |           }
424 |         } else if (n.isThis()) {
425 |           parametersReferenced.add(THIS_MARKER);
426 |         }
427 |       }

===== M0392 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0396 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.has(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2736-2754
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2736 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2737 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2738 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2739 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2740 |       return true;
2741 |     }
2742 | 
2743 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2744 |       return false;
2745 |     }
2746 | 
2747 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2748 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2749 |         return true;
2750 |       }
2751 |     }
2752 | 
2753 |     return false;
2754 |   }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.isSupportedCallType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 220-235
Documentation:
/**
   * Only ".call" calls and direct calls to functions are supported.
   * @param callNode The call evaluate.
   * @return Whether the call is of a type that is supported.
   */
Method code:
220 |   private boolean isSupportedCallType(Node callNode) {
221 |     if (!callNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
222 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
223 |         if (!assumeStrictThis) {
224 |           Node thisValue = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
225 |           if (thisValue == null || !thisValue.isThis()) {
226 |             return false;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode)) {
230 |         return false;
231 |       }
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     return true;
235 |   }

===== M0411 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 796-809
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
796 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
797 |     if (item == null) {
798 |       return null;
799 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
800 |       return item.getNext();
801 |     } else {
802 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
803 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
804 |         return item.chain(result);
805 |       } else {
806 |         return item;
807 |       }
808 |     }
809 |   }

/**
   * @return "expression" or the node closest to "expression", that does not
   * have a conditional ancestor.
   */
Method code:
253 |   private static Node findNonconditionalParent(
254 |       Node subExpression, Node expressionRoot) {
255 |      Node result = subExpression;
256 | 
257 |      for (Node child = subExpression, parent = child.getParent();
258 |           parent != expressionRoot;
259 |           child = parent, parent = child.getParent()) {
260 |        if (isConditionalOp(parent)) {
261 |          // Only the first child is always executed, if the function may never
262 |          // be called, don't inline it.
263 |          if (child != parent.getFirstChild()) {
264 |            result = parent;
265 |          }
266 |        }
267 |      }
268 | 
269 |      return result;
270 |   }

Original range: 3346-3380
Documentation:
/** Checks that the scope roots marked as changed have indeed changed */
Method code:
3346 |   public static void verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node> map,
3347 |       Node main, boolean verifyUnchangedNodes,
3348 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
3349 |     // compiler is passed only to call compiler.toSource during debugging to see
3350 |     // mismatches in scopes
3351 | 
3352 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is false, we are comparing the initial AST to the
3353 |     // final AST. Don't check unmarked nodes b/c they may have been changed by
3354 |     // non-loopable passes.
3355 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is true, we are comparing the ASTs before & after
3356 |     // a pass. Check all scope roots.
3357 |     final Map<Node, Node> mtoc = map;
3358 |     final boolean checkUnchanged = verifyUnchangedNodes;
3359 |     Node clone = mtoc.get(main);
3360 |     if (main.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3361 |       Preconditions.checkState(!main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3362 |     } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3363 |       Preconditions.checkState(main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3364 |     }
3365 |     visitPreOrder(main,
3366 |         new Visitor() {
3367 |           @Override
3368 |           public void visit(Node n) {
3369 |             if (n.isFunction() && mtoc.containsKey(n)) {
3370 |               Node clone = mtoc.get(n);
3371 |               if (n.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3372 |                 Preconditions.checkState(!n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3373 |               } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3374 |                 Preconditions.checkState(n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3375 |               }
3376 |             }
3377 |           }
3378 |         },
3379 |         Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
3380 |   }

/**
   * This is similar to NodeUtil.isLValue except that object properties and
   * array member modification aren't important ("o" in "o.a = 2" is still "o"

     * @return Whether the node may have non-local side-effects.
     */
Method code:
433 |     private boolean hasNonLocalSideEffect(Node n) {
434 |       boolean sideEffect = false;
435 |       int type = n.getType();
436 |       // Note: Only care about changes to non-local names, specifically
437 |       // ignore VAR declaration assignments.
438 |       if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n)
439 |           || type == Token.INC
440 |           || type == Token.DEC) {
441 |         Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
442 |         // Ignore changes to local names.

445 |         }

449 |         sideEffect = NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n);

451 |         sideEffect = true;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.rewriteFunctionDeclarations(Node)

Original range: 121-143

/**
   * @param n The node to inspect
   */
Method code:
121 |   private void rewriteFunctionDeclarations(Node n) {
122 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
123 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
124 |         // Rewrite: function f() {} ==> var f = function() {}
125 |         Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
126 | 
127 |         Node name = IR.name(fnNameNode.getString()).srcref(fnNameNode);
128 |         Node var = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
129 | 
130 |         fnNameNode.setString("");
131 |         // Add the VAR, remove the FUNCTION
132 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, var);
133 |         // readd the function as a function expression
134 |         name.addChildToFront(n);
135 |       }
136 |       return;
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(), next; c != null; c = next) {
140 |       next = c.getNext(); // We may rewrite "c"
141 |       rewriteFunctionDeclarations(c);
142 |     }
143 |   }

===== M0431 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 173-202
Documentation:

Method code:
173 |     public void findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
174 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
175 |         // There aren't any interesting functions here.
176 |         return;
177 |       }
178 | 
179 |       switch (n.getType()) {
180 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
181 |         //   var fooFn = function(x) { return ... }
182 |         case Token.VAR:
183 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
184 |           Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
185 |           if (nameNode.isName() && nameNode.hasChildren()
186 |               && nameNode.getFirstChild().isFunction()) {
187 |             maybeAddFunction(new FunctionVar(n), t.getModule());
188 |           }
189 |           break;
190 | 
191 |         // Named functions
192 |         // function Foo(x) { return ... }
193 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
194 |           Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)
195 |               || parent.isLabel());
196 |           if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
197 |             Function fn = new NamedFunction(n);
198 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
199 |           }
200 |           break;
201 |       }
202 |     }

===== M0439 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 241-277
Documentation:

Method code:
241 |   void addNumber(double x) {
242 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
243 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
244 |     char prev = getLastChar();
245 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
246 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
247 |       add(" ");
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     if (negativeZero) {
251 |       addConstant("-0");
252 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
253 |       long value = (long) x;
254 |       long mantissa = value;
255 |       int exp = 0;
256 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
257 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
258 |           mantissa /= 10;
259 |           exp++;
260 |         }
261 |       }
262 |       if (exp > 2) {
263 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
264 |       } else {
265 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
266 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
267 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
268 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
269 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
270 |         } else {
271 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
272 |         }
273 |       }
274 |     } else {
275 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
276 |     }
277 |   }

===== M0445 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

===== M0450 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.isSubexpressionMovable(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 746-836
Documentation:
/**
   * Walk the AST from the call site to the expression root and verify that
   * the portions of the expression that are evaluated before the call are:
   * 1) Unaffected by the the side-effects, if any, of the call.
   * 2) That there are no side-effects, that may influence the call.
   *
   * For example, if x has side-effects:
   *   a = 1 + x();
   * the call to x can be moved because "a" final value of a can not be
   * influenced by x(), but in:
   *   a = b + x();
   * the call to x can not be moved because the value of b may be modified
   * by the call to x.
   *
   * If x is without side-effects in:
   *   a = b + x();
   * the call to x can be moved, but in:
   *   a = (b.foo = c) + x();
   * the call to x can not be moved because the value of b.foo may be referenced
   * by x().  Note: this is true even if b is a local variable; the object that
   * b refers to may have a global alias.
   *
   * @return UNDECOMPOSABLE if the expression can not be moved, DECOMPOSABLE if
   * decomposition is required before the expression can be moved, otherwise
   * MOVABLE.
   */
Method code:
746 |   private DecompositionType isSubexpressionMovable(
747 |       Node expressionRoot, Node subExpression) {
748 |     boolean requiresDecomposition = false;
749 |     boolean seenSideEffects = NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(
750 |         subExpression, compiler);
751 | 
752 |     Node child = subExpression;
753 |     for (Node parent : child.getAncestors()) {
754 |       if (parent == expressionRoot) {
755 |         // Done. The walk back to the root of the expression is complete, and
756 |         // nothing was encountered that blocks the call from being moved.
757 |         return requiresDecomposition
758 |             ? DecompositionType.DECOMPOSABLE
759 |             : DecompositionType.MOVABLE;
760 |       }
761 | 
762 |       if (isConditionalOp(parent)) {
763 |         // Only the first child is always executed, otherwise it must be
764 |         // decomposed.
765 |         if (child != parent.getFirstChild()) {
766 |           requiresDecomposition = true;
767 |         }
768 |       } else {
769 |         // Only inline the call if none of the preceding siblings in the
770 |         // expression have side-effects, and are unaffected by the side-effects,
771 |         // if any, of the call in question.
772 |         // NOTE: This depends on the siblings being in the same order as they
773 |         // are evaluated.
774 | 
775 |         // SPECIAL CASE: Assignment to a simple name
776 |         if (isSafeAssign(parent, seenSideEffects)) {
777 |           // It is always safe to inline "foo()" for expressions such as
778 |           //   "a = b = c = foo();"
779 |           // As the assignment is unaffected by side effect of "foo()"
780 |           // and the names assigned-to can not influence the state before
781 |           // the call to foo.
782 |           //
783 |           // This is not true of more complex LHS values, such as
784 |           //    a.x = foo();
785 |           //    next().x = foo();
786 |           // in these cases the checks below are necessary.
787 |         } else {
788 |           // Everything else.
789 |           for (Node n : parent.children()) {
790 |             if (n == child) {
791 |               // None of the preceding siblings have side-effects.
792 |               // This is OK.
793 |               break;
794 |             }
795 | 
796 |             if (isExpressionTreeUnsafe(
797 |                 n, seenSideEffects)) {
798 |               seenSideEffects = true;
799 |               requiresDecomposition = true;
800 |             }
801 |           }
802 | 
803 |           // In Internet Explorer, DOM objects and other external objects
804 |           // methods can not be called indirectly, as is required when the
805 |           // object or its property can be side-effected.  For example,
806 |           // when exposing expression f() (with side-effects) in: x.m(f())
807 |           // either the value of x or its property m might have changed, so
808 |           // both the 'this' value ('x') and the function to be called ('x.m')
809 |           // need to be preserved. Like so:
810 |           //   var t1 = x, t2 = x.m, t3 = f();
811 |           //   t2.call(t1, t3);
812 |           // As IE doesn't support the call to these non-JavaScript objects
813 |           // methods in this way. We can't do this.
814 |           // We don't currently distinguish between these types of objects
815 |           // in the extern definitions and if we did we would need accurate
816 |           // type information.
817 |           //
818 |           Node first = parent.getFirstChild();
819 |           if (requiresDecomposition
820 |               && parent.isCall()
821 |               && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
822 |             if (maybeExternMethod(first)) {
823 |               return DecompositionType.UNDECOMPOSABLE;
824 |             } else {
825 |               return DecompositionType.DECOMPOSABLE;
826 |             }
827 |           }
828 |         }
829 |       }
830 |       // Continue looking up the expression tree.
831 |       child = parent;
832 |     }
833 | 
834 |     // With a valid tree we should never get here.
835 |     throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected.");
836 |   }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(String,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 119-159
Documentation:
/**
   * @param fnName The name of this function. This either the name of the
   *  variable to which the function is assigned or the name from the FUNCTION
   *  node.
   * @param fnNode The FUNCTION node of the function to inspect.
   * @return Whether the function node meets the minimum requirements for
   * inlining.
   */
Method code:
119 |   boolean doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(
120 |       final String fnName, Node fnNode) {
121 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
122 | 
123 |     // Basic restrictions on functions that can be inlined:
124 |     // 0) The function is inlinable by convention
125 |     // 1) It contains a reference to itself.
126 |     // 2) It uses its parameters indirectly using "arguments" (it isn't
127 |     //    handled yet.
128 |     // 3) It references "eval". Inline a function containing eval can have
129 |     //    large performance implications.
130 | 
131 |     if (!compiler.getCodingConvention().isInlinableFunction(fnNode)) {
132 |       return false;
133 |     }
134 | 
135 |     final String fnRecursionName = fnNode.getFirstChild().getString();
136 |     Preconditions.checkState(fnRecursionName != null);
137 | 
138 |     // If the function references "arguments" directly in the function
139 |     boolean referencesArguments = NodeUtil.isNameReferenced(
140 |         block, "arguments", NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION);
141 | 
142 |     // or it references "eval" or one of its names anywhere.
143 |     Predicate<Node> p = new Predicate<Node>(){
144 |       @Override
145 |       public boolean apply(Node n) {
146 |         if (n.isName()) {
147 |           return n.getString().equals("eval")
148 |             || (!fnName.isEmpty()
149 |                 && n.getString().equals(fnName))
150 |             || (!fnRecursionName.isEmpty()
151 |                 && n.getString().equals(fnRecursionName));
152 |         }
153 |         return false;
154 |       }
155 |     };
156 | 
157 |     return !referencesArguments
158 |         && !NodeUtil.has(block, p, Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
159 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;

533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {

535 |       }

537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 548-561
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
548 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
549 |       Node ret;
550 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
551 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
552 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
553 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
554 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
555 |       } else {
556 |         ret = transform(n);
557 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
558 |       }
559 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
560 |       return ret;
561 |     }

===== M0488 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(FunctionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 742-768
Documentation:
/**
   * @see #resolveInlineConflicts
   */
Method code:
742 |   private void resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(FunctionState fs) {
743 |     // Functions that aren't referenced don't cause conflicts.
744 |     if (!fs.hasReferences() || !fs.canInline()) {
745 |       return;
746 |     }
747 | 
748 |     Node fnNode = fs.getFn().getFunctionNode();
749 |     Set<String> names = findCalledFunctions(fnNode);
750 |     if (!names.isEmpty()) {
751 |       // Prevent the removal of the referenced functions.
752 |       for (String name : names) {
753 |         FunctionState fsCalled = fns.get(name);
754 |         if (fsCalled != null && fsCalled.canRemove()) {
755 |           fsCalled.setRemove(false);
756 |           // For functions that can no longer be removed, check if they should
757 |           // still be inlined.
758 |           if (!mimimizeCost(fsCalled)) {
759 |             // It can't be inlined remove it from the list.
760 |             fsCalled.setInline(false);
761 |           }
762 |         }
763 |       }
764 | 
765 |       // Make a copy of the Node, so it isn't changed by other inlines.
766 |       fs.setSafeFnNode(fs.getFn().getFunctionNode().cloneTree());
767 |     }
768 |   }

===== M0517 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3087-3115
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3087 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3088 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3089 |     if (info == null) {
3090 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3091 |       if (parent == null) {
3092 |         return null;
3093 |       }
3094 | 
3095 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3096 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3097 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3098 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3099 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3100 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3101 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3102 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3103 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3104 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3105 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3106 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3107 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3108 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3109 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3110 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3111 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3112 |       }
3113 |     }
3114 |     return info;
3115 |   }

===== M0518 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeChild(Node)

===== M0520 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineFunction(Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 455-536
Documentation:
/**
   * Inline a function which fulfills the requirements of
   * canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock into the call site, replacing the
   * parent expression.
   */
Method code:
455 |   private Node inlineFunction(
456 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode, String fnName) {
457 |     Node parent = callNode.getParent();
458 |     Node grandParent = parent.getParent();
459 | 
460 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Consider storing the callSite classification in the
461 |     // reference object and passing it in here.
462 |     CallSiteType callSiteType = classifyCallSite(callNode);
463 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(callSiteType != CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED);
464 | 
465 |     boolean isCallInLoop = NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(callNode);
466 | 
467 |     // Store the name for the result. This will be used to
468 |     // replace "return expr" statements with "resultName = expr"
469 |     // to replace
470 |     String resultName = null;
471 |     boolean needsDefaultReturnResult = true;
472 |     switch (callSiteType) {
473 |       case SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
474 |         resultName = parent.getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         break;
476 | 
477 |       case VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
478 |         resultName = parent.getString();
479 |         break;
480 | 
481 |       case SIMPLE_CALL:
482 |         resultName = null;  // "foo()" doesn't need a result.
483 |         needsDefaultReturnResult = false;
484 |         break;
485 | 
486 |       case EXPRESSION:
487 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
488 |             "Movable expressions must be moved before inlining.");
489 | 
490 |       case DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION:
491 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
492 |             "Decomposable expressions must be decomposed before inlining.");
493 | 
494 |       default:
495 |         throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call site type.");
496 |     }
497 | 
498 |     FunctionToBlockMutator mutator = new FunctionToBlockMutator(
499 |         compiler, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
500 | 
501 |     Node newBlock = mutator.mutate(
502 |         fnName, fnNode, callNode, resultName,
503 |         needsDefaultReturnResult, isCallInLoop);
504 | 
505 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Create a common mutation function that
506 |     // can replace either a VAR name assignment, assignment expression or
507 |     // a EXPR_RESULT.
508 |     Node greatGrandParent = grandParent.getParent();
509 |     switch (callSiteType) {
510 |       case VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
511 |         // Remove the call from the name node.
512 |         parent.removeChild(parent.getFirstChild());
513 |         Preconditions.checkState(parent.getFirstChild() == null);
514 |         // Add the call, after the VAR.
515 |         greatGrandParent.addChildAfter(newBlock, grandParent);
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
519 |         // The assignment is now part of the inline function so
520 |         // replace it completely.
521 |         Preconditions.checkState(grandParent.isExprResult());
522 |         greatGrandParent.replaceChild(grandParent, newBlock);
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case SIMPLE_CALL:
526 |         // If nothing is looking at the result just replace the call.
527 |         Preconditions.checkState(parent.isExprResult());
528 |         grandParent.replaceChild(parent, newBlock);
529 |         break;
530 | 
531 |       default:
532 |         throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call site type.");
533 |     }
534 | 
535 |     return newBlock;
536 |   }

===== M0524 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1232-1234
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1232 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1233 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1234 |   }

170 |     @Override
171 |     boolean isEquivalentTo(
172 |         Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
173 |       boolean equiv = super.isEquivalentTo(node, compareJsType, recur, shallow);
174 |       if (equiv) {

177 |         if (thisValue == thatValue) {

===== M0534 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.getReplacementReturnStatement(Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 434-460
Documentation:
/**
   * Replace the 'return' statement with its child expression.
   * If the result is needed (resultName != null):
   *   "return foo()" becomes "resultName = foo()"
   *   "return" becomes "resultName = void 0".
   * Otherwise:
   *   "return foo()" becomes "foo()"
   *   "return", null is returned.
   */
Method code:
434 |   private static Node getReplacementReturnStatement(
435 |       Node node, String resultName) {
436 |     Node resultNode = null;
437 | 
438 |     Node retVal = null;
439 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
440 |       // Clone the child as the child hasn't been removed
441 |       // from the node yet.
442 |       retVal = node.getFirstChild().cloneTree();
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     if (resultName == null) {
446 |       if (retVal != null) {
447 |         resultNode = NodeUtil.newExpr(retVal); // maybe null.
448 |       }
449 |     } else {
450 |       if (retVal == null) {
451 |         // A result is needed create a dummy value.
452 |         Node srcLocation = node;
453 |         retVal = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation);
454 |       }
455 |       // Create a "resultName = retVal;" statement.
456 |       resultNode = createAssignStatementNode(resultName, retVal);
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     return resultNode;
460 |   }

===== M0541 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 157-261
Documentation:

Method code:
157 |     @Override
158 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
159 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
160 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
161 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
162 |       if (inExterns && node.isName() && parent.isParamList()) {
163 |         return;
164 |       }
165 | 
166 |       Definition def =
167 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
168 |       if (def != null) {
169 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
170 |         if (name != null) {
171 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
172 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
173 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
174 |               !rValue.isFunction()) {
175 | 
176 |             // Unhandled complex expression
177 |             Definition unknownDef =
178 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
179 |             def = unknownDef;
180 |           }
181 | 
182 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
183 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
184 |           if (inExterns) {
185 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
186 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
187 | 
188 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
189 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
190 | 
191 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
192 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
193 |             // literal key.
194 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
195 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
196 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
197 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
198 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
199 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
200 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
201 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
202 |                   }
203 |                 }
204 |               }
205 | 
206 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
207 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
208 |               }
209 |             }
210 |           }
211 | 
212 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
213 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
214 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
215 |                                                    def,
216 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
217 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
218 |                                                    inExterns));
219 |         }
220 |       }
221 | 
222 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && parent.isExprResult()) {
223 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
224 |         if (name != null) {
225 | 
226 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
227 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
228 | 
229 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
230 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
231 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
232 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
233 | 
234 |           boolean dropStub = false;
235 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
236 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
237 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
238 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
239 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
240 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
241 |                   dropStub = true;
242 |                   break;
243 |                 }
244 |               }
245 |             }
246 |           }
247 | 
248 |           if (!dropStub) {
249 |             // Incomplete definition
250 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
251 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
252 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
253 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
254 |                                                      definition,
255 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
256 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
257 |                                                      inExterns));
258 |           }
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |     }

   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.

212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,

226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);

214 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
215 |     char prev = getLastChar();
216 | 
217 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
218 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
219 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
220 |       append(" ");
221 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
222 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
223 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
224 |       append(" ");
225 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
226 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
227 |       append(" ");
228 |     }
229 | 
230 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
231 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
232 | 
233 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
234 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
235 |     // because it looks strange.
236 |     if (binOp) {
237 |       maybeCutLine();
238 |     }
239 |   }
