/**
   * Replace the 'return' statement with its child expression.
   *   "return foo()" becomes "foo()" or "resultName = foo()"
   *   "return" is removed or becomes "resultName = void 0".
   *
   * @param block
   * @param resultName
   */
Method code:
399 |   private static void convertLastReturnToStatement(
400 |       Node block, String resultName) {
401 |     Node ret = block.getLastChild();
402 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ret.isReturn());

404 | 
405 |     if (resultNode == null) {
406 |       block.removeChild(ret);
407 |     } else {

409 |       block.replaceChild(ret, resultNode);
410 |     }

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.isSafeAssign(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 859-873
Documentation:
/**
   * It is always safe to inline "foo()" for expressions such as
   *    "a = b = c = foo();"
   * As the assignment is unaffected by side effect of "foo()"
   * and the names assigned-to can not influence the state before
   * the call to foo.
   *
   * It is also safe in cases like where the object is constant:
   *    CONST_NAME.a = foo()
   *    CONST_NAME[CONST_VALUE] = foo();
   *
   * This is not true of more complex LHS values, such as
   *     a.x = foo();
   *     next().x = foo();
   * in these cases the checks below are necessary.
   *
   * @param seenSideEffects If true, check to see if node-tree maybe affected by
   * side-effects, otherwise if the tree has side-effects. @see
   * isExpressionTreeUnsafe
   * @return Whether the assignment is safe from side-effects.
   */
Method code:
859 |   private boolean isSafeAssign(Node n, boolean seenSideEffects) {
860 |     if (n.isAssign()) {
861 |       Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
862 |       switch (lhs.getType()) {
863 |         case Token.NAME:
864 |           return true;
865 |         case Token.GETPROP:
866 |           return !isExpressionTreeUnsafe(lhs.getFirstChild(), seenSideEffects);
867 |         case Token.GETELEM:
868 |           return !isExpressionTreeUnsafe(lhs.getFirstChild(), seenSideEffects)
869 |               && !isExpressionTreeUnsafe(lhs.getLastChild(), seenSideEffects);
870 |       }
871 |     }
872 |     return false;
873 |   }

===== M0564 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.canBeSideEffected(Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1139-1171
Documentation:
/**
   * @param knownConstants A set of names known to be constant value at
   * node 'n' (such as locals that are last written before n can execute).
   * @return Whether the tree can be affected by side-effects or
   * has side-effects.
   */
Method code:
1139 |   static boolean canBeSideEffected(Node n, Set<String> knownConstants) {
1140 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1141 |       case Token.CALL:
1142 |       case Token.NEW:
1143 |         // Function calls or constructor can reference changed values.
1144 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Add some mechanism for determining that functions
1145 |         // are unaffected by side effects.
1146 |         return true;
1147 |       case Token.NAME:
1148 |         // Non-constant names values may have been changed.
1149 |         return !isConstantName(n)
1150 |             && !knownConstants.contains(n.getString());
1151 | 
1152 |       // Properties on constant NAMEs can still be side-effected.
1153 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1154 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1155 |         return true;
1156 | 
1157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1158 |         // Function expression are not changed by side-effects,
1159 |         // and function declarations are not part of expressions.
1160 |         Preconditions.checkState(isFunctionExpression(n));
1161 |         return false;
1162 |     }
1163 | 
1164 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
1165 |       if (canBeSideEffected(c, knownConstants)) {
1166 |         return true;
1167 |       }
1168 |     }
1169 | 
1170 |     return false;
1171 |   }

===== M0567 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.checkNameUsage(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 547-586
Documentation:
/**
     * Find functions that can be inlined.
     */
Method code:
547 |     private void checkNameUsage(Node n, Node parent) {
548 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isName());
549 | 
550 |       if (isCandidateUsage(n)) {
551 |         return;
552 |       }
553 | 
554 |       // Other refs to a function name remove its candidacy for inlining
555 |       String name = n.getString();
556 |       FunctionState fs = fns.get(name);
557 |       if (fs == null) {
558 |         return;
559 |       }
560 | 
561 |       // Unlike normal call/new parameters, references passed to
562 |       // JSCompiler_ObjectPropertyString are not aliases of a value, but
563 |       // a reference to the name itself, as such the value of the name is
564 |       // unknown and can not be inlined.
565 |       if (parent.isNew()) {
566 |         Node target = parent.getFirstChild();
567 |         if (target.isName() && target.getString().equals(
568 |             ObjectPropertyStringPreprocess.EXTERN_OBJECT_PROPERTY_STRING)) {
569 |           // This method is going to be replaced so don't inline it anywhere.
570 |           fs.setInline(false);
571 |         }
572 |       }
573 | 
574 |       // If the name is being assigned to it can not be inlined.
575 |       if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
576 |         // e.g. bar = something; <== we can't inline "bar"
577 |         // so mark the function as uninlinable.
578 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Should we just remove it from fns here?
579 |         fs.setInline(false);
580 |       } else {
581 |         // e.g. var fn = bar; <== we can't inline "bar"
582 |         // As this reference can't be inlined mark the function as
583 |         // unremovable.
584 |         fs.setRemove(false);
585 |       }
586 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1575-1590
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1575 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1576 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1577 |       String name = getString();
1578 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1579 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1580 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1581 |       if (left == null) {
1582 |         return null;
1583 |       }
1584 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1585 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1586 |       return "this";
1587 |     } else {
1588 |       return null;
1589 |     }
1590 |   }

Original range: 119-159

Method code:
119 |     @Override
120 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
121 |       if (!inExterns && hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
122 |         traversal.report(node, INVALID_NO_SIDE_EFFECT_ANNOTATION);
123 |       }
124 | 
125 |       if (node.isGetProp()) {
126 |         if (parent.isExprResult() &&
127 |             hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
128 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.add(node);
129 |         }
130 |       } else if (node.isFunction()) {
131 | 
132 |         // The annotation may attached to the function node, the
133 |         // variable declaration or assignment expression.
134 |         boolean hasAnnotation = hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node);
135 |         List<Node> nameNodes = Lists.newArrayList();
136 |         nameNodes.add(node.getFirstChild());
137 | 
138 |         if (parent.isName()) {
139 |           Node gramp = parent.getParent();
140 |           if (gramp.isVar() &&
141 |               gramp.hasOneChild() &&
142 |               hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(gramp)) {
143 |             hasAnnotation = true;
144 |           }
145 | 
146 |           nameNodes.add(parent);
147 |         } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
148 |           if (hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(parent)) {
149 |             hasAnnotation = true;
150 |           }
151 | 
152 |           nameNodes.add(parent.getFirstChild());
153 |         }
154 | 
155 |         if (hasAnnotation) {
156 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.addAll(nameNodes);
157 |         }
158 |       }
159 |     }

===== M0590 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 136-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
136 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
137 |     if (node.isName()) {
138 |       String name = node.getString();
139 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
140 |         return name;
141 |       } else {
142 |         return null;
143 |       }
144 |     } else if (node.isGetProp()) {
145 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
146 |     }
147 |     return null;
148 |   }

644 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
645 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
646 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
647 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
648 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 384-417
Documentation:

Method code:
384 |     @Override
385 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
386 |       switch (n.getType()) {
387 |         case Token.WHILE:
388 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
389 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
390 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
391 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
392 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
393 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
394 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
395 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
396 |           }
397 |           break;
398 | 
399 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
400 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
401 |           break;
402 | 
403 |         case Token.NAME:
404 |         case Token.STRING:
405 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
406 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
407 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
408 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
409 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
410 |           }
411 |           break;
412 | 
413 |         case Token.CAST:
414 |           parent.replaceChild(n, n.removeFirstChild());
415 |           break;
416 |       }
417 |     }

117 |     static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {

===== M0606 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateUsage(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 425-459
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the name is used in a way that might be a candidate
   *   for inlining.
   */
Method code:
425 |   static boolean isCandidateUsage(Node name) {
426 |     Node parent = name.getParent();
427 |     Preconditions.checkState(name.isName());
428 |     if (parent.isVar() || parent.isFunction()) {
429 |       // This is a declaration.  Duplicate declarations are handle during
430 |       // function candidate gathering.
431 |       return true;
432 |     }
433 | 
434 |     if (parent.isCall() && parent.getFirstChild() == name) {
435 |       // This is a normal reference to the function.
436 |       return true;
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Check for a ".call" to the named function:
440 |     //   CALL
441 |     //     GETPROP/GETELEM
442 |     //       NAME
443 |     //       STRING == "call"
444 |     //     This-Value
445 |     //     Function-parameter-1
446 |     //     ...
447 |     if (NodeUtil.isGet(parent)
448 |          && name == parent.getFirstChild()
449 |          && name.getNext().isString()
450 |          && name.getNext().getString().equals("call")) {
451 |       Node gramps = name.getAncestor(2);
452 |       if (gramps.isCall()
453 |           && gramps.getFirstChild() == parent) {
454 |         // Yep, a ".call".
455 |         return true;
456 |       }
457 |     }
458 |     return false;
459 |   }

===== M0611 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 239-270
Documentation:

Method code:
239 |     @Override
240 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
241 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
242 |       if (n.isName()) {
243 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
244 |           return;
245 |         }
246 | 
247 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
248 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top-level variable.
249 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
250 |         if (var != null) {
251 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
255 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
256 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
257 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
258 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
259 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
260 |           if (assertOnChange) {
261 |             String name = n.getString();
262 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
263 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
264 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
265 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
266 |           }
267 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
268 |         }
269 |       }
270 |     }

===== M0615 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 520-548
Documentation:

Method code:
520 |     @Override
521 |     void endFile() {
522 |       super.endFile();
523 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
524 |         return;
525 |       }
526 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
527 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
528 |         append(";");
529 |         startNewLine();
530 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
531 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
532 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
533 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
534 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
535 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
536 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
537 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
538 |         lineIndex--;
539 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
540 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
541 |         append(";");
542 |         startNewLine();
543 |       } else {
544 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
545 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
546 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
547 |       }
548 |     }

===== M0627 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 81-110
Documentation:

Method code:
81 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
82 |     int startErrorCount = compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount();
83 |     try {
84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null ||
97 |         // Most passes try to report as many errors as possible,
98 |         // so there may already be errors. We only care if there were
99 |         // errors in the code we just parsed.
100 |         (compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount() > startErrorCount && !compiler.isIdeMode())) {
101 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
102 |       root = IR.script();
103 |     } else {
104 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
108 |     // the source file and module.
109 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
110 |   }

===== M0641 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1429-1465
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1429 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1430 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1431 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1432 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1433 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1434 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1435 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1436 |           continue;
1437 |         }
1438 |       }
1439 | 
1440 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1441 | 
1442 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1443 |       if (n == null) {
1444 |         continue;
1445 |       }
1446 | 
1447 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1448 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1449 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1450 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1451 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1452 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1453 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1454 | 
1455 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1456 | 
1457 |         externs.add(input);
1458 |         staleInputs = true;
1459 |       }
1460 |     }
1461 | 
1462 |     if (staleInputs) {
1463 |       repartitionInputs();
1464 |     }
1465 |   }

===== M0655 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 914-934
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the Node is a DO or FUNCTION (with or without
   * labels).
   */
Method code:
914 |   private boolean isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node n) {
915 |     if (n.isLabel()) {
916 |       Node labeledStatement = n.getLastChild();
917 |       if (!labeledStatement.isBlock()) {
918 |         return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(labeledStatement);
919 |       } else {
920 |         // For labels with block children, we need to ensure that a
921 |         // labeled FUNCTION or DO isn't generated when extraneous BLOCKs
922 |         // are skipped.
923 |         if (getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2) == 1) {
924 |           return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(getFirstNonEmptyChild(n));
925 |         } else {
926 |           // Either a empty statement or an block with more than one child,
927 |           // way it isn't a FUNCTION or DO.
928 |           return false;
929 |         }
930 |       }
931 |     } else {
932 |       return (n.isFunction() || n.isDo());
933 |     }
934 |   }

===== M0656 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 157-179
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
158 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
159 | 
160 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
161 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
162 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 | 
164 |       // ignore cast nodes.
165 |       while (first.isCast()) {
166 |         first = first.getFirstChild();
167 |       }
168 | 
169 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
170 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
174 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
175 |       if (first.isName() &&
176 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
177 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
178 |       }
179 |     }

===== M0662 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inliningLowersCost(JSModule,Node,Collection<? extends Reference>,Set<String>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 764-820
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine if inlining the function is likely to reduce the code size.
   * @param namesToAlias
   */
Method code:
764 |   boolean inliningLowersCost(
765 |       JSModule fnModule, Node fnNode, Collection<? extends Reference> refs,
766 |       Set<String> namesToAlias, boolean isRemovable, boolean referencesThis) {
767 |     int referenceCount = refs.size();
768 |     if (referenceCount == 0) {
769 |       return true;
770 |     }
771 | 
772 |     int referencesUsingBlockInlining = 0;
773 | 
774 |     boolean checkModules = isRemovable && fnModule != null;
775 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
776 | 
777 |     for (Reference ref : refs) {
778 |       if (ref.mode == InliningMode.BLOCK) {
779 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining++;
780 |       }
781 | 
782 |       // Check if any of the references cross the module boundaries.
783 |       if (checkModules && ref.module != null) {
784 |         if (ref.module != fnModule &&
785 |             !moduleGraph.dependsOn(ref.module, fnModule)) {
786 |           // Calculate the cost as if the function were non-removable,
787 |           // if it still lowers the cost inline it.
788 |           isRemovable = false;
789 |           checkModules = false;  // no need to check additional modules.
790 |         }
791 |       }
792 |     }
793 | 
794 |     int referencesUsingDirectInlining = referenceCount -
795 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining;
796 | 
797 |     // Don't bother calculating the cost of function for simple functions where
798 |     // possible.
799 |     // However, when inlining a complex function, even a single reference may be
800 |     // larger than the original function if there are many returns (resulting
801 |     // in additional assignments) or many parameters that need to be aliased
802 |     // so use the cost estimating.
803 |     if (referenceCount == 1 && isRemovable &&
804 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining == 1) {
805 |       return true;
806 |     }
807 | 
808 |     int callCost = estimateCallCost(fnNode, referencesThis);
809 |     int overallCallCost = callCost * referenceCount;
810 | 
811 |     int costDeltaDirect = inlineCostDelta(
812 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.DIRECT);
813 |     int costDeltaBlock = inlineCostDelta(
814 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.BLOCK);
815 | 
816 |     return doesLowerCost(fnNode, overallCallCost,
817 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining, costDeltaDirect,
818 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining, costDeltaBlock,
819 |         isRemovable);
820 |   }

===== M0676 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 579-603
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * There are several criteria that the function and reference must hold in
   * order for the functions to be inlined:
   * - It must be a simple call, or assignment, or var initialization.
   * <pre>
   *    f();
   *    a = foo();
   *    var a = foo();
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
579 |   private CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(
580 |       NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias) {
581 |     CallSiteType callSiteType = classifyCallSite(callNode);
582 |     if (callSiteType == CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED) {
583 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
584 |     }
585 | 
586 |     if (!allowDecomposition
587 |         && (callSiteType == CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION
588 |             || callSiteType == CallSiteType.EXPRESSION)) {
589 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
590 |     }
591 | 
592 |     if (!callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(
593 |             t, callNode, fnNode, namesToAlias)) {
594 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
595 |     }
596 | 
597 |     if (callSiteType == CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION
598 |         || callSiteType == CallSiteType.EXPRESSION) {
599 |       return CanInlineResult.AFTER_PREPARATION;
600 |     } else {
601 |       return CanInlineResult.YES;
602 |     }
603 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1514-1565
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1514 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(
1515 |       Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
1516 |     if (type != node.getType()
1517 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1518 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1519 |       return false;
1520 |     }
1521 | 
1522 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1523 |       return false;
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1527 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1528 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1529 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1530 |         return false;
1531 |       }
1532 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1533 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1534 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1535 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1536 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1537 |           return false;
1538 |         }
1539 |       }
1540 | 
1541 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1542 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1543 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1544 |         return false;
1545 |       }
1546 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1547 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1548 |         return false;
1549 |       }
1550 |     }
1551 | 
1552 |     if (recur) {
1553 |       Node n, n2;
1554 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1555 |            n != null;
1556 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1557 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1558 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1559 |           return false;
1560 |         }
1561 |       }
1562 |     }
1563 | 
1564 |     return true;
1565 |   }

121 |   @Override
122 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
123 |     new NodeTraversal(
124 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
125 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
126 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
127 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
128 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
129 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
130 |     }
131 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
132 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
133 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
134 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
135 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
136 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
137 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
138 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
139 |     //   function f() {
140 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
141 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
142 |     //   }
143 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
144 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
145 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
146 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
147 |         .process(externs, root);
148 |

150 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
151 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
152 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
153 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
154 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
155 |     }
156 |

158 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
159 |     }

===== M0690 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceToFunction(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>,InliningMode,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 173-213
Documentation:
/**
   * @param t  The traversal use to reach the call site.
   * @param callNode The CALL node.
   * @param fnNode The function to evaluate for inlining.
   * @param needAliases A set of function parameter names that can not be
   *     used without aliasing. Returned by getUnsafeParameterNames().
   * @param mode Inlining mode to be used.
   * @param referencesThis Whether fnNode contains references to its this
   *     object.
   * @param containsFunctions Whether fnNode contains inner functions.
   * @return Whether the inlining can occur.
   */
Method code:
173 |   CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceToFunction(NodeTraversal t,
174 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> needAliases,
175 |       InliningMode mode, boolean referencesThis, boolean containsFunctions) {
176 |     // TODO(johnlenz): This function takes too many parameter, without
177 |     // context.  Modify the API to take a structure describing the function.
178 | 
179 |     // Allow direct function calls or "fn.call" style calls.
180 |     if (!isSupportedCallType(callNode)) {
181 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
182 |     }
183 | 
184 |     // Limit where functions that contain functions can be inline.  Introducing
185 |     // an inner function into another function can capture a variable and cause
186 |     // a memory leak.  This isn't a problem in the global scope as those values
187 |     // last until explicitly cleared.
188 |     if (containsFunctions) {
189 |       if (!assumeMinimumCapture && !t.inGlobalScope()) {
190 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Allow inlining into any scope without local names or
191 |         // inner functions.
192 |         return CanInlineResult.NO;
193 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(callNode)) {
194 |         // An inner closure maybe relying on a local value holding a value for a
195 |         // single iteration through a loop.
196 |         return CanInlineResult.NO;
197 |       }
198 |     }
199 | 
200 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Add support for 'apply'
201 |     if (referencesThis && !NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
202 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Allow 'this' references to be replaced with a
203 |       // global 'this' object.
204 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
205 |     }
206 | 
207 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
208 |       return canInlineReferenceDirectly(callNode, fnNode);
209 |     } else {
210 |       return canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(
211 |           t, callNode, fnNode, needAliases);
212 |     }
213 |   }

602 |     @Override

604 |         NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, Node parent, FunctionState fs) {
605 |       Preconditions.checkState(fs.hasExistingFunctionDefinition());
606 |       if (fs.canInline()) {
607 |         Reference ref = fs.getReference(callNode);
608 |         // There are two cases ref can be null: if the call site was introduce
609 |         // because it was part of a function that was inlined during this pass
610 |         // or if the call site was trimmed from the list of references because
611 |         // the function couldn't be inlined at this location.
612 |         if (ref != null) {
613 |           if (specializationState != null) {
614 |             Node containingFunction = getContainingFunction(t);
615 | 
616 |             if (containingFunction != null) {
617 |               // Report that the function was specialized so that
618 |               // {@link SpecializeModule} can fix it up.
619 |               specializationState.reportSpecializedFunction(containingFunction);
620 |             }
621 |           }
622 | 
623 |           inlineFunction(t, callNode, fs, ref.mode);
624 |           // Keep track of references that have been inlined so that
625 |           // we can verify that none have been missed.
626 |           ref.inlined = true;
627 |         }
628 |       }
629 |     }

===== M0695 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesLowerCost(Node,int,int,int,int,int,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 825-853
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether inlining will lower cost.
   */
Method code:
825 |   private boolean doesLowerCost(
826 |       Node fnNode, int callCost,
827 |       int directInlines, int costDeltaDirect,
828 |       int blockInlines, int costDeltaBlock,
829 |       boolean removable) {
830 | 
831 |     // Determine the threshold value for this inequality:
832 |     //     inline_cost < call_cost
833 |     // But solve it for the function declaration size so the size of it
834 |     // is only calculated once and terminated early if possible.
835 | 
836 |     int fnInstanceCount = directInlines + blockInlines - (removable ? 1 : 0);
837 |     // Prevent division by zero.
838 |     if (fnInstanceCount == 0) {
839 |       // Special case single reference function that are being block inlined:
840 |       // If the cost of the inline is greater than the function definition size,
841 |       // don't inline.
842 |       if (blockInlines > 0 && costDeltaBlock > 0) {
843 |         return false;
844 |       }
845 |       return true;
846 |     }
847 | 
848 |     int costDelta = (directInlines * costDeltaDirect) +
849 |         (blockInlines * costDeltaBlock);
850 |     int threshold = (callCost - costDelta) / fnInstanceCount;
851 | 
852 |     return InlineCostEstimator.getCost(fnNode, threshold + 1) <= threshold;
853 |   }

===== M0699 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 37-65
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
37 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
38 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
39 |     // can be simplified.
40 | 
41 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
42 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
43 |     if (parent == null) {
44 |       return null;
45 |     }
46 | 
47 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
48 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
49 |     } else if (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
50 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
51 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
52 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
53 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
54 |       }
55 |     } else if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
56 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
57 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n)) {
58 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
59 |           isExtern);
60 |     } else if (parent.isParamList()) {
61 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
62 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
63 |     }
64 |     return null;
65 |   }

===== M0700 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1195-1215
Documentation:

Method code:
1195 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1196 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1197 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1198 |       return s;
1199 |     }
1200 | 
1201 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1202 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1203 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1204 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1205 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1206 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1207 |       // crazy.
1208 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1209 |         sb.append(c);
1210 |       } else {
1211 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1212 |       }
1213 |     }
1214 |     return sb.toString();
1215 |   }

===== M0708 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 112-166
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
112 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
113 |     switch (n.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.VAR:
115 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
116 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
117 |              child != null;) {
118 |           Node next = child.getNext();
119 |           declareVar(child);
120 |           child = next;
121 |         }
122 |         return;
123 | 
124 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
125 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
126 |           return;
127 |         }
128 | 
129 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
130 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
131 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
135 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
136 | 
137 |       case Token.CATCH:
138 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
139 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
140 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
141 |         // is the code block
142 | 
143 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
144 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
145 | 
146 |         declareVar(var);
147 |         scanVars(block);
148 |         return;  // only one child to scan
149 | 
150 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
151 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
152 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
153 |         break;
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
157 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
159 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
160 |            child != null;) {
161 |         Node next = child.getNext();
162 |         scanVars(child);
163 |         child = next;
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

===== M0711 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 766-848
Documentation:

Method code:
766 |     @Override
767 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
768 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
769 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
770 |       if (name == null) {
771 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
772 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
773 |           errorReporter.error(
774 |             "unnamed function statement",
775 |             sourceName,
776 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
777 | 
778 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
779 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
780 |         }
781 |         name = new Name();
782 |         name.setIdentifier("");
783 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
784 |       }
785 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
786 |       Node newName = transform(name);
787 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
788 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
789 |         // declaration.
790 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
791 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
792 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
793 |         // same line as the function keyword.
794 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
795 |             functionNode.getLp();
796 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
797 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
798 |       }
799 | 
800 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
801 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
802 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
803 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
804 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
805 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
806 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
807 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
808 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
809 |       if (fnName != null) {
810 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
811 |       } else {
812 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
813 |       }
814 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
815 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
816 | 
817 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
818 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
819 |         Node paramNode = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(param);
820 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
821 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
822 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
823 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
824 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
825 |         } else {
826 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
827 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
828 |           // (an error has already been reported).
829 |           Preconditions.checkState(
830 |               config.isIdeMode
831 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
832 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
833 |         }
834 |       }
835 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
836 | 
837 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
838 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
839 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
840 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
841 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
842 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
843 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
844 |       }
845 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
846 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
847 |      return node;
848 |     }

===== M0718 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateFunction(Function)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 333-359
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function.
   */
Method code:
333 |   private boolean isCandidateFunction(Function fn) {
334 |     // Don't inline exported functions.
335 |     String fnName = fn.getName();
336 |     if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(fnName)) {
337 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Should we allow internal references to be inlined?
338 |       // An exported name can be replaced externally, any inlined instance
339 |       // would not reflect this change.
340 |       // To allow inlining we need to be able to distinguish between exports
341 |       // that are used in a read-only fashion and those that can be replaced
342 |       // by external definitions.
343 |       return false;
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     // Don't inline this special function
347 |     if (RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(fnName)) {
348 |       return false;
349 |     }
350 | 
351 |     // Don't inline if we are specializing and the function can't be fixed up
352 |     if (specializationState != null &&
353 |         !specializationState.canFixupFunction(fn.getFunctionNode())) {
354 |       return false;
355 |     }
356 | 
357 |     Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
358 |     return injector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(fnName, fnNode);
359 |   }

===== M0722 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1974-1976
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1974 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1975 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1976 |   }

===== M0723 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceDirectly(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 689-758
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * There are several criteria that the function and reference must hold in
   * order for the functions to be inlined:
   * 1) If a call's arguments have side effects,
   * the corresponding argument in the function must only be referenced once.
   * For instance, this will not be inlined:
   * <pre>
   *     function foo(a) { return a + a }
   *     x = foo(i++);
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
689 |   private CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceDirectly(
690 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode) {
691 |     if (!isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(fnNode)) {
692 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
693 |     }
694 | 
695 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
696 | 
697 |     boolean hasSideEffects = false;
698 |     if (block.hasChildren()) {
699 |       Preconditions.checkState(block.hasOneChild());
700 |       Node stmt = block.getFirstChild();
701 |       if (stmt.isReturn()) {
702 |         hasSideEffects = NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(stmt.getFirstChild(), compiler);
703 |       }
704 |     }
705 |     // CALL NODE: [ NAME, ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
706 |     Node cArg = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
707 | 
708 |     // Functions called via 'call' and 'apply' have a this-object as
709 |     // the first parameter, but this is not part of the called function's
710 |     // parameter list.
711 |     if (!callNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
712 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
713 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Support replace this with a value.
714 |         if (cArg == null || !cArg.isThis()) {
715 |           return CanInlineResult.NO;
716 |         }
717 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
718 |       } else {
719 |         // ".apply" call should be filtered before this.
720 |         Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode));
721 |       }
722 |     }
723 | 
724 |     // FUNCTION NODE -> LP NODE: [ ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
725 |     Node fnParam = NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(fnNode).getFirstChild();
726 |     while (cArg != null || fnParam != null) {
727 |       // For each named parameter check if a mutable argument use more than one.
728 |       if (fnParam != null) {
729 |         if (cArg != null) {
730 |           if (hasSideEffects && NodeUtil.canBeSideEffected(cArg)) {
731 |             return CanInlineResult.NO;
732 |           }
733 |           // Check for arguments that are evaluated more than once.
734 |           // Note: Unlike block inlining, there it is not possible that a
735 |           // parameter reference will be in a loop.
736 |           if (NodeUtil.mayEffectMutableState(cArg, compiler)
737 |               && NodeUtil.getNameReferenceCount(
738 |                   block, fnParam.getString()) > 1) {
739 |             return CanInlineResult.NO;
740 |           }
741 |         }
742 | 
743 |         // Move to the next name.
744 |         fnParam = fnParam.getNext();
745 |       }
746 | 
747 |       // For every call argument check for side-effects, even if there
748 |       // isn't a named parameter to match.
749 |       if (cArg != null) {
750 |         if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(cArg, compiler)) {
751 |           return CanInlineResult.NO;
752 |         }
753 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
754 |       }
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     return CanInlineResult.YES;
758 |   }

===== M0729 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(Node,Node,Set<String>,Set<String>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 194-218
Documentation:
/**
   * Check for uses of the named value that imply a pass-by-value
   * parameter is expected.  This is used to prevent cases like:
   *
   *   function (x) {
   *     x=2;
   *     return x;
   *   }
   *
   * We don't want "undefined" to be substituted for "x", and get
   *   undefined=2
   *
   * @param n The node in question.
   * @param parent The parent of the node.
   * @param names The set of names to check.
   * @param unsafe The set of names that require aliases.
   * @param inInnerFunction Whether the inspection is occurring on a inner
   *     function.
   */
Method code:
194 |   private static Set<String> findModifiedParameters(
195 |       Node n, Node parent, Set<String> names, Set<String> unsafe,
196 |       boolean inInnerFunction) {
197 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(unsafe != null);
198 |     if (n.isName()) {
199 |       if (names.contains(n.getString())) {
200 |         if (inInnerFunction || canNameValueChange(n, parent)) {
201 |           unsafe.add(n.getString());
202 |         }
203 |       }
204 |     } else if (n.isFunction()) {
205 |       // A function parameter can not be replaced with a direct inlined value
206 |       // if it is referred to by an inner function. The inner function
207 |       // can out live the call we are replacing, so inner function must
208 |       // capture a unique name.  This approach does not work within loop
209 |       // bodies so those are forbidden elsewhere.
210 |       inInnerFunction = true;
211 |     }
212 | 
213 |     for (Node c : n.children()) {
214 |       findModifiedParameters(c, n, names, unsafe, inInnerFunction);
215 |     }
216 | 
217 |     return unsafe;
218 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 946-1005
Documentation:

Method code:
946 |   private void toString(
947 |       StringBuilder sb,
948 |       boolean printSource,
949 |       boolean printAnnotations,
950 |       boolean printType) {
951 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
952 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
953 |       sb.append(' ');
954 |       sb.append(getString());
955 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
956 |       sb.append(' ');
957 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
958 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
959 |       // toStringTree.
960 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
961 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
962 |       } else {
963 |         sb.append(first.getString());
964 |       }
965 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
966 |       sb.append(' ');
967 |       sb.append(getDouble());
968 |     }
969 |     if (printSource) {
970 |       int lineno = getLineno();
971 |       if (lineno != -1) {
972 |         sb.append(' ');
973 |         sb.append(lineno);
974 |       }
975 |     }
976 | 
977 |     if (printAnnotations) {
978 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
979 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
980 |         int type = keys[i];
981 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
982 |         sb.append(" [");
983 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
984 |         sb.append(": ");
985 |         String value;
986 |         switch (type) {
987 |           default:
988 |             value = x.toString();
989 |             break;
990 |         }
991 |         sb.append(value);
992 |         sb.append(']');
993 |       }
994 |     }
995 | 
996 |     if (printType) {
997 |       if (jsType != null) {
998 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
999 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1000 |           sb.append(" : ");
1001 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1002 |         }
1003 |       }
1004 |     }
1005 |   }

===== M0767 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExpressionDecomposer.findExpressionRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExpressionDecomposer.java
Original range: 657-685
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The statement containing the expression. null if subExpression
   *     is not contain by in by a Node where inlining is known to be possible.
   *     For example, a WHILE node condition expression.
   */
Method code:
657 |   static Node findExpressionRoot(Node subExpression) {
658 |     Node child = subExpression;
659 |     for (Node parent : child.getAncestors()) {
660 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
661 |       switch (parentType) {
662 |         // Supported expression roots:
663 |         // SWITCH and IF can have multiple children, but the CASE, DEFAULT,
664 |         // or BLOCK will be encountered first for any of the children other
665 |         // than the condition.
666 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
667 |         case Token.IF:
668 |         case Token.SWITCH:
669 |         case Token.RETURN:
670 |         case Token.VAR:
671 |           Preconditions.checkState(child == parent.getFirstChild());
672 |           return parent;
673 |         // Any of these indicate an unsupported expression:
674 |         case Token.SCRIPT:
675 |         case Token.BLOCK:
676 |         case Token.LABEL:
677 |         case Token.CASE:
678 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
679 |           return null;
680 |       }
681 |       child = parent;
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected AST structure.");
685 |   }

===== M0771 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 567-614
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private void extractForInitializer(
568 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
569 | 
570 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
571 |         next = c.getNext();
572 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
573 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
574 |         switch (c.getType()) {
575 |           case Token.LABEL:
576 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
577 |             break;
578 |           case Token.FOR:
579 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
580 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
581 |               if (first.isVar()) {
582 |                 // Transform:
583 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
584 |                 // to:
585 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
586 |                 Node newStatement = first;
587 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
588 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
589 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
590 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
591 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
592 |               }
593 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
594 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
595 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
596 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
597 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
598 | 
599 |               Node newStatement;
600 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
601 |               // but are handled differently.
602 |               if (init.isVar()) {
603 |                 newStatement = init;
604 |               } else {
605 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
606 |               }
607 | 
608 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
609 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
610 |             }
611 |             break;
612 |         }
613 |       }
614 |     }

/**
   * @param n The node with which to start iterating.
   * @param stopNode A node after which to stop iterating.
   */
Method code:
300 |   private void decomposeSubExpressions(
301 |       Node n, Node stopNode, DecompositionState state) {
302 |     if (n == null || n == stopNode) {
303 |       return;
304 |     }
305 | 
306 |     // Never try to decompose an object literal key.
307 |     Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n));
308 |

310 |     // determining if the any of the children following have side-effects.
311 |     // If they do we need to be more aggressive about removing values
312 |     // from the expression.
313 |     decomposeSubExpressions(

318 |     if (isExpressionTreeUnsafe(n, state.sideEffects)) {

320 |       // side-effects.
321 |       state.sideEffects = true;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1300-1424
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1300 |   Node parseInputs() {
1301 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1302 | 
1303 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1304 |     // individual file parse trees.
1305 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1306 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1307 |     }
1308 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1309 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1310 |     }
1311 | 
1312 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1313 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1314 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1315 | 
1316 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1317 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1318 | 
1319 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1320 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1321 | 
1322 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1323 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1324 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1325 |     }
1326 | 
1327 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1328 | 
1329 |     try {
1330 |       // Parse externs sources.
1331 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1332 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1333 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1334 |           return null;
1335 |         }
1336 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1337 |       }
1338 | 
1339 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1340 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1341 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1342 |       }
1343 | 
1344 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1345 | 
1346 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1347 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1348 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1349 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1350 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1351 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1352 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1353 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1354 |           }
1355 |         }
1356 | 
1357 |         try {
1358 |           inputs =
1359 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1360 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1361 |           staleInputs = true;
1362 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1363 |           report(JSError.make(
1364 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1365 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1366 |           report(JSError.make(
1367 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1368 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1369 |           report(JSError.make(
1370 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1371 |         }
1372 | 
1373 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1374 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1375 |           return null;
1376 |         }
1377 |       }
1378 | 
1379 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1380 | 
1381 |       if (staleInputs) {
1382 |         repartitionInputs();
1383 |       }
1384 | 
1385 |       // Build the AST.
1386 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1387 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1388 |         if (n == null) {
1389 |           continue;
1390 |         }
1391 | 
1392 |         if (devMode) {
1393 |           runSanityCheck();
1394 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1395 |             return null;
1396 |           }
1397 |         }
1398 | 
1399 |         // TODO(johnlenz): we shouldn't need to check both isExternExportsEnabled and
1400 |         // externExportsPath.
1401 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1402 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1403 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1404 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1405 | 
1406 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1407 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1408 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1409 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1410 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1411 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1412 |         }
1413 | 
1414 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1415 |       }
1416 | 
1417 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1418 |         return null;
1419 |       }
1420 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1421 |     } finally {
1422 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1423 |     }
1424 |   }

===== M0787 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.resolveInlineConflicts()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 733-737
Documentation:
/**
   * Size base inlining calculations are thrown off when a function that is
   * being inlined also contains calls to functions that are slated for
   * inlining.
   *
   * Specifically, a clone of the FUNCTION node tree is used when the function
   * is inlined. Calls in this new tree are not included in the list of function
   * references so they won't be inlined (which is what we want). Here we mark
   * those functions as non-removable (as they will have new references in the
   * cloned node trees).
   *
   * This prevents a function that would only be inlined because it is
   * referenced once from being inlined into multiple call sites because
   * the calling function has been inlined in multiple locations or the
   * function being removed while there are still references.
   */
Method code:
733 |   private void resolveInlineConflicts() {
734 |     for (FunctionState fs : fns.values()) {
735 |       resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(fs);
736 |     }
737 |   }

116 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.

===== M0794 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }

===== M0809 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyStatement(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 852-908
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
852 |   private void addNonEmptyStatement(
853 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
854 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
855 | 
856 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && !n.isBlock()) {
857 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
858 |     }
859 | 
860 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
861 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
862 |     if (n.isBlock()) {
863 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2);
864 |       if (count == 0) {
865 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
866 |           cc.beginBlock();
867 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
868 |         } else {
869 |           cc.endStatement(true);
870 |         }
871 |         return;
872 |       }
873 | 
874 |       if (count == 1) {
875 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
876 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
877 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
878 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
879 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
880 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock || isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(firstAndOnlyChild)) {
881 |           cc.beginBlock();
882 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
883 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
884 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
885 |           return;
886 |         } else {
887 |           // Continue with the only child.
888 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
889 |         }
890 |       }
891 | 
892 |       if (count > 1) {
893 |         context = Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
894 |       }
895 |     }
896 | 
897 |     if (nodeToProcess.isEmpty()) {
898 |       cc.endStatement(true);
899 |     } else {
900 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
901 | 
902 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
903 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
904 |       if (nodeToProcess.isVar()) {
905 |         cc.endStatement();
906 |       }
907 |     }
908 |   }

===== M0819 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 624-641
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
624 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
625 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
626 |         next = c.getNext();
627 |         if (c.isVar()) {
628 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
629 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
630 |           }
631 | 
632 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
633 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
634 |             c.removeChild(name);
635 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
636 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
637 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
638 |           }
639 |         }
640 |       }
641 |     }

===== M0830 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.mutate(String,Node,Node,String,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 64-115
Documentation:
/**
   * @param fnName The name to use when preparing human readable names.
   * @param fnNode The function to prepare.
   * @param callNode The call node that will be replaced.
   * @param resultName Function results should be assigned to this name.
   * @param needsDefaultResult Whether the result value must be set.
   * @param isCallInLoop Whether the function body must be prepared to be
   *   injected into the body of a loop.
   * @return A clone of the function body mutated to be suitable for injection
   *   as a statement into another code block.
   */
Method code:
64 |   Node mutate(String fnName, Node fnNode, Node callNode,
65 |       String resultName, boolean needsDefaultResult, boolean isCallInLoop) {
66 |     Node newFnNode = fnNode.cloneTree();
67 |     // Now that parameter names have been replaced, make sure all the local
68 |     // names are unique, to allow functions to be inlined multiple times
69 |     // without causing conflicts.
70 |     makeLocalNamesUnique(newFnNode, isCallInLoop);
71 | 
72 |     // Function declarations must be rewritten as function expressions as
73 |     // they will be within a block and normalization prevents function
74 |     // declarations within block as browser implementations vary.
75 |     rewriteFunctionDeclarations(newFnNode.getLastChild());

78 |     // modified.

82 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(
83 |             newFnNode, callNode, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
84 |     boolean hasArgs = !args.isEmpty();
85 |     if (hasArgs) {
86 |       FunctionArgumentInjector.maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(
87 |           newFnNode, args, namesToAlias, compiler.getCodingConvention());
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     Node newBlock = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(newFnNode);
91 |     // Make the newBlock insertable .
92 |     newBlock.detachFromParent();
93 | 
94 |     if (hasArgs) {
95 |       Node inlineResult = aliasAndInlineArguments(newBlock,
96 |           args, namesToAlias);
97 |       Preconditions.checkState(newBlock == inlineResult);
98 |     }
99 | 
100 |     //
101 |     // For calls inlined into loops, VAR declarations are not reinitialized to
102 |     // undefined as they would have been if the function were called, so ensure
103 |     // that they are properly initialized.
104 |     //
105 |     if (isCallInLoop) {
106 |       fixUnitializedVarDeclarations(newBlock);
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     String labelName = getLabelNameForFunction(fnName);
110 |     Node injectableBlock = replaceReturns(
111 |         newBlock, resultName, labelName, needsDefaultResult);
112 |     Preconditions.checkState(injectableBlock != null);
113 | 
114 |     return injectableBlock;
115 |   }

     * shouldTraverse is call when descending into the Node tree, so it is used

147 |     @Override
148 |     public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node node,
149 |         Node parent) {
150 |       if (node.isLabel()) {
151 |         // Determine the new name for this label.
152 |         LabelNamespace current = namespaceStack.peek();
153 |         int currentDepth = current.renameMap.size() + 1;
154 |         String name = node.getFirstChild().getString();
155 | 
156 |         // Store the context for this label name.
157 |         LabelInfo li = new LabelInfo(currentDepth);
158 |         Preconditions.checkState(!current.renameMap.containsKey(name));
159 |         current.renameMap.put(name, li);
160 | 
161 |         // Create a new name, if needed, for this depth.
162 |         if (names.size() < currentDepth) {
163 |           names.add(nameSupplier.get());
164 |         }
165 | 
166 |         String newName = getNameForId(currentDepth);
167 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("label renamed: " + name + " => " + newName);
168 |       }
169 | 
170 |       return true;
171 |     }

===== M0834 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(Node,Map<String, Node>,Set<String>,CodingConvention)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 245-314
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the set of parameter names in set unsafe to include any
   * arguments from the call site that require aliases.
   * @param fnNode The FUNCTION node to be inlined.
   * @param argMap The argument list for the call to fnNode.
   * @param namesNeedingTemps The set of names to update.
   */
Method code:
245 |   static void maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(
246 |       Node fnNode, Map<String, Node> argMap, Set<String> namesNeedingTemps,
247 |       CodingConvention convention) {
248 |     if (argMap.isEmpty()) {
249 |       // No arguments to check, we are done.
250 |       return;
251 |     }
252 | 
253 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(fnNode.isFunction());
254 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |     Set<String> parameters = argMap.keySet();
257 | 
258 |     // Get the list of parameters that may need temporaries due to
259 |     // side-effects.
260 |     Set<String> namesAfterSideEffects = findParametersReferencedAfterSideEffect(
261 |         parameters, block);
262 | 
263 |     // Check for arguments that are evaluated more than once.
264 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Node> entry : argMap.entrySet()) {
265 |       String argName = entry.getKey();
266 |       if (namesNeedingTemps.contains(argName)) {
267 |         continue;
268 |       }
269 |       Node cArg = entry.getValue();
270 |       boolean safe = true;
271 |       int references = NodeUtil.getNameReferenceCount(block, argName);
272 | 
273 |       if (NodeUtil.mayEffectMutableState(cArg) && references > 0) {
274 |         // Note: Mutable arguments should be assigned to temps, as the
275 |         // may be within in a loop:
276 |         //   function x(a) {
277 |         //     for(var i=0; i<0; i++) {
278 |         //       foo(a);
279 |         //     }
280 |         //   x( [] );
281 |         //
282 |         //   The parameter in the call to foo should not become "[]".
283 |         safe = false;
284 |       } else if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(cArg)) {
285 |         // Even if there are no references, we still need to evaluate the
286 |         // expression if it has side-effects.
287 |         safe = false;
288 |       } else if (NodeUtil.canBeSideEffected(cArg)
289 |           && namesAfterSideEffects.contains(argName)) {
290 |         safe = false;
291 |       } else if (references > 1) {
292 |         // Safe is a misnomer, this is a check for "large".
293 |         switch (cArg.getType()) {
294 |           case Token.NAME:
295 |             String name = cArg.getString();
296 |             safe = !(convention.isExported(name));
297 |             break;
298 |           case Token.THIS:
299 |             safe = true;
300 |             break;
301 |           case Token.STRING:
302 |             safe = (cArg.getString().length() < 2);
303 |             break;
304 |           default:
305 |             safe = NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(cArg);
306 |             break;
307 |         }
308 |       }
309 | 
310 |       if (!safe) {
311 |         namesNeedingTemps.add(argName);
312 |       }
313 |     }
314 |   }
