Method code:
93 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
94 |     switch (n.getType()) {
95 |       case Token.LABEL:
96 |         validateLabel(n);
97 |         return;
98 |       case Token.BLOCK:
99 |         validateBlock(n);
100 |         return;
101 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
102 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
103 |         return;
104 |       case Token.WITH:
105 |         validateWith(n);
106 |         return;
107 |       case Token.FOR:
108 |         validateFor(n);
109 |         return;
110 |       case Token.WHILE:
111 |         validateWhile(n);
112 |         return;
113 |       case Token.DO:
114 |         validateDo(n);
115 |         return;
116 |       case Token.SWITCH:
117 |         validateSwitch(n);
118 |         return;
119 |       case Token.IF:
120 |         validateIf(n);
121 |         return;
122 |       case Token.VAR:
123 |         validateVar(n);
124 |         return;
125 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
126 |         validateExprStmt(n);
127 |         return;
128 |       case Token.RETURN:
129 |         validateReturn(n);
130 |         return;
131 |       case Token.THROW:
132 |         validateThrow(n);
133 |         return;
134 |       case Token.TRY:
135 |         validateTry(n);
136 |         return;
137 |       case Token.BREAK:
138 |         validateBreak(n);
139 |         return;
140 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
141 |         validateContinue(n);
142 |         return;
143 |       case Token.EMPTY:
144 |         validateChildless(n);
145 |         return;
146 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
147 |         validateChildless(n);
148 |         return;
149 |       default:

151 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);

===== M0838 =====

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 107-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
113 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
118 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
119 | 
120 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
121 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
122 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
123 | 
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
147 |   }

===== M0847 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 75-96
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   @Override
76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used, the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
85 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
86 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
87 |     } else {
88 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     if (!declarationRoot.isFunction()) {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0864 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 298-321
Documentation:

Method code:
298 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
299 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
300 |       return;
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     try {
304 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
305 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
306 | 
307 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
308 |       sourceName = "";
309 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
310 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
311 | 
312 |       for (Node root : roots) {
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
314 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
315 |       }
316 | 
317 |       popScope();
318 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
319 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
320 |     }
321 |   }

===== M0865 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.maybeAddFunction(Function,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 240-306
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the FunctionState object for the given function. Checks if the
   * given function matches the criteria for an inlinable function.
   */
Method code:
240 |   private void maybeAddFunction(Function fn, JSModule module) {
241 |     String name = fn.getName();
242 |     FunctionState fs = getOrCreateFunctionState(name);
243 | 
244 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe "smarten" FunctionState by adding this logic
245 |     // to it?
246 | 
247 |     // If the function has multiple definitions, don't inline it.
248 |     if (fs.hasExistingFunctionDefinition()) {
249 |       fs.setInline(false);
250 |     } else {
251 |       // verify the function hasn't already been marked as "don't inline"
252 |       if (fs.canInline()) {
253 |         // store it for use when inlining.
254 |         fs.setFn(fn);
255 |         if (injector.isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(
256 |             fn.getFunctionNode())) {
257 |           fs.inlineDirectly(true);
258 |         }
259 | 
260 |         // verify the function meets all the requirements.
261 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Minimum requirement checks are about 5% of the
262 |         // run-time cost of this pass.
263 |         if (!isCandidateFunction(fn)) {
264 |           // It doesn't meet the requirements.
265 |           fs.setInline(false);
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         // Set the module and gather names that need temporaries.
269 |         if (fs.canInline()) {
270 |           fs.setModule(module);
271 | 
272 |           Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
273 |           Set<String> namesToAlias =
274 |               FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(fnNode);
275 |           if (!namesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
276 |             fs.inlineDirectly(false);
277 |             fs.setNamesToAlias(namesToAlias);
278 |           }
279 | 
280 |           Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
281 |           if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(block)) {
282 |             fs.setReferencesThis(true);
283 |           }
284 | 
285 |           if (NodeUtil.containsFunction(block)) {
286 |             fs.setHasInnerFunctions(true);
287 |             // If there are inner functions, we can inline into global scope
288 |             // if there are no local vars or named functions.
289 |             // TODO(johnlenz): this can be improved by looking at the possible
290 |             // values for locals.  If there are simple values, or constants
291 |             // we could still inline.
292 |             if (!assumeMinimumCapture && hasLocalNames(fnNode)) {
293 |               fs.setInline(false);
294 |             }
295 |           }
296 |         }
297 | 
298 |         // Check if block inlining is allowed.
299 |         if (fs.canInline() && !fs.canInlineDirectly()) {
300 |           if (!blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
301 |             fs.setInline(false);
302 |           }
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |     }
306 |   }

===== M0875 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1190-1197
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1190 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1191 |     int precedence = precedenceWithDefault(type);
1192 |     if (precedence != -1) {
1193 |       return precedence;
1194 |     }
1195 |     throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1196 |         Token.name(type) + " (type " + type + ")");
1197 |   }

===== M0882 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 630-642
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
630 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
631 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
632 |     if (typeSet == null) {
633 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
634 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
638 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
639 | 
640 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
641 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
642 |   }

===== M0890 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 491-515
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
491 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
492 |     switch (n.getType()) {
493 |       case Token.STRING:
494 |       case Token.NUMBER:
495 |       case Token.NULL:
496 |       case Token.TRUE:
497 |       case Token.FALSE:
498 |         return true;
499 |       case Token.CAST:
500 |       case Token.NOT:
501 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
502 |       case Token.VOID:
503 |       case Token.NEG:
504 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
505 |       case Token.NAME:
506 |         String name = n.getString();
507 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
508 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
509 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
510 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
511 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
512 |     }
513 | 
514 |     return false;
515 |   }

===== M0894 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

===== M0895 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 632-646
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
632 |   public Scope getScope() {
633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
639 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
640 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
641 |       scopes.push(scope);
642 |     }
643 |     scopeRoots.clear();
644 |     // No need to call compiler.setScope; the top scopeRoot is now the top scope
645 |     return scope;
646 |   }

/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
496 |     @Override
497 |     void maybeCutLine() {
498 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
499 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
500 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
501 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
502 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
503 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
504 |           prevCutPosition = position;
505 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
506 |           lineIndex++;
507 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
508 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
509 |         } else {
510 |           startNewLine();
511 |         }
512 |       }
513 |     }

Method code:
299 |     @Override
300 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
301 |       if (n.isName()) {
302 |         String name = n.getString();
303 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
304 |           return;
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
308 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
309 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
310 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
311 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
312 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
313 |             expectedConst = true;
314 |           } else {
315 |             expectedConst = false;
316 | 
317 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
318 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
319 |             if (var != null) {
320 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
321 |             }
322 | 
323 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
324 |               expectedConst = true;
325 |             } else {
326 |               expectedConst = false;
327 |             }
328 |           }
329 | 
330 |           if (expectedConst) {
331 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
332 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
333 |           } else {
334 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
335 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
336 |           }
337 |         }
338 | 
339 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
340 |         if (value == null) {
341 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
342 |         } else {
343 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
344 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
345 |         }
346 |       }
347 |     }

===== M0909 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 647-679
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
647 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
648 |       Preconditions.checkState(
649 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
650 |       Node previous = null;
651 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
652 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
653 |       // are already in the right place.
654 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
655 |         previous = current;
656 |         current = current.getNext();
657 |       }
658 | 
659 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
660 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
661 |       while (current != null) {
662 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
663 |         Node next = current.getNext();
664 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
665 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
666 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
667 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
668 | 
669 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
670 |           // previous declarations).
671 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
672 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
673 |         } else {
674 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
675 |           previous = current;
676 |         }
677 |         current = next;
678 |       }
679 |     }

===== M0917 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

===== M0923 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 542-560
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function, and if so, adds it to our set of inlinable functions.
   */
Method code:
542 |   boolean isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(Node fnNode) {
543 |     // Only inline single-statement functions
544 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
545 | 
546 |     // Check if this function is suitable for direct replacement of a CALL node:
547 |     // a function that consists of single return that returns an expression.
548 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
549 |       // special case empty functions.
550 |       return true;
551 |     } else if (block.hasOneChild()) {
552 |       // Only inline functions that return something.
553 |       if (block.getFirstChild().isReturn()
554 |           && block.getFirstChild().getFirstChild() != null) {
555 |         return true;
556 |       }
557 |     }
558 | 
559 |     return false;
560 |   }

===== M0971 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

===== M0979 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.classifyCallSite(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 394-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine which, if any, of the supported types the call site is.
   */
Method code:
394 |   private CallSiteType classifyCallSite(Node callNode) {
395 |     Node parent = callNode.getParent();
396 |     Node grandParent = parent.getParent();
397 | 
398 |     // Verify the call site:
399 |     if (NodeUtil.isExprCall(parent)) {
400 |       // This is a simple call?  Example: "foo();".
401 |       return CallSiteType.SIMPLE_CALL;
402 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isExprAssign(grandParent)
403 |         && !NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(callNode, parent)
404 |         && parent.getFirstChild().isName()
405 |         && !NodeUtil.isConstantName(parent.getFirstChild())) {
406 |       // This is a simple assignment.  Example: "x = foo();"
407 |       return CallSiteType.SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT;
408 |     } else if (parent.isName()
409 |         && !NodeUtil.isConstantName(parent)
410 |         && grandParent.isVar()
411 |         && grandParent.hasOneChild()) {
412 |       // This is a var declaration.  Example: "var x = foo();"
413 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Should we be checking for constants on the
414 |       // left-hand-side of the assignments and handling them as EXPRESSION?
415 |       return CallSiteType.VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT;
416 |     } else {
417 |       Node expressionRoot = ExpressionDecomposer.findExpressionRoot(callNode);
418 |       if (expressionRoot != null) {
419 |         ExpressionDecomposer decomposer = new ExpressionDecomposer(
420 |             compiler, safeNameIdSupplier, knownConstants);
421 |         DecompositionType type = decomposer.canExposeExpression(
422 |             callNode);
423 |         if (type == DecompositionType.MOVABLE) {
424 |           return CallSiteType.EXPRESSION;
425 |         } else if (type == DecompositionType.DECOMPOSABLE) {
426 |           return CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION;
427 |         } else {
428 |           Preconditions.checkState(type == DecompositionType.UNDECOMPOSABLE);
429 |         }
430 |       }
431 |     }
432 | 
433 |     return CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED;
434 |   }

===== M0987 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.replaceReturns(Node,String,String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 323-369
Documentation:
/**
   *  Convert returns to assignments and breaks, as needed.
   *  For example, with a labelName of 'foo':
   *    {
   *      return a;
   *    }
   *  becomes:
   *    foo: {
   *      a;
   *      break foo;
   *    }
   *  or
   *    foo: {
   *      resultName = a;
   *      break foo;
   *    }
   *
   * @param resultMustBeSet Whether the result must always be set to a value.
   * @return The node containing the transformed block, this may be different
   *     than the passed in node 'block'.
   */
Method code:
323 |   private static Node replaceReturns(
324 |       Node block, String resultName, String labelName,
325 |       boolean resultMustBeSet) {
326 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(block);
327 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(labelName);
328 | 
329 |     Node root = block;
330 | 
331 |     boolean hasReturnAtExit = false;
332 |     int returnCount = NodeUtil.getNodeTypeReferenceCount(
333 |         block, Token.RETURN, new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
334 |     if (returnCount > 0) {
335 |       hasReturnAtExit = hasReturnAtExit(block);
336 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Simpler not to special case this,
337 |       // and let it be optimized later.
338 |       if (hasReturnAtExit) {
339 |         convertLastReturnToStatement(block, resultName);
340 |         returnCount--;
341 |       }
342 | 
343 |       if (returnCount > 0) {
344 |         // A label and breaks are needed.
345 | 
346 |         // Add the breaks
347 |         replaceReturnWithBreak(block, null, resultName, labelName);
348 | 
349 |         // Add label
350 |         Node name = IR.labelName(labelName).srcref(block);
351 |         Node label = IR.label(name, block).srcref(block);
352 | 
353 |         Node newRoot = IR.block().srcref(block);
354 |         newRoot.addChildrenToBack(label);
355 | 
356 | 
357 |         // The label is now the root.
358 |         root = newRoot;
359 |       }
360 |     }
361 | 
362 |     // If there wasn't an return at the end of the function block, and we need
363 |     // a result, add one to the block.
364 |     if (resultMustBeSet && !hasReturnAtExit && resultName != null) {
365 |       addDummyAssignment(block, resultName);
366 |     }
367 | 
368 |     return root;
369 |   }

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {

299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;

305 |       } else {

===== M0990 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1000-1062
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
1000 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
1001 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
1002 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
1003 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
1004 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
1005 |     }
1006 | 
1007 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
1008 |       return false;
1009 |     }
1010 | 
1011 |     if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesArgumentsCall() &&
1012 |         allArgsUnescapedLocal(callNode)) {
1013 |       return false;
1014 |     }
1015 | 
1016 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
1017 | 
1018 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1019 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1020 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1021 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1022 |         return false;
1023 |       }
1024 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1025 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1026 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1027 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1028 |         return false;
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1032 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1033 |         return false;
1034 |       }
1035 | 
1036 |       // Math.floor has no side-effects.
1037 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1038 |       // I create a definitionProvider that understands namespacing.
1039 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1040 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1041 |           return false;
1042 |         }
1043 |       }
1044 | 
1045 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1046 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1047 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1048 |           return false;
1049 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1050 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1051 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1052 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1053 |           if (param != null &&
1054 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp())) {
1055 |             return false;
1056 |           }
1057 |         }
1058 |       }
1059 |     }
1060 | 
1061 |     return true;
1062 |   }

===== M0997 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal,FunctionState,Node,JSModule)

Original range: 488-513
Documentation:

Method code:
488 |     void maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal t, FunctionState fs,
489 |         Node callNode, JSModule module) {
490 |       if (!fs.canInline()) {
491 |         return;
492 |       }
493 | 
494 |       boolean referenceAdded = false;
495 |       InliningMode mode = fs.canInlineDirectly()
496 |            ? InliningMode.DIRECT : InliningMode.BLOCK;
497 |       referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
498 |           t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
499 |       if (!referenceAdded &&
500 |           mode == InliningMode.DIRECT && blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
501 |         // This reference can not be directly inlined, see if
502 |         // block replacement inlining is possible.
503 |         mode = InliningMode.BLOCK;
504 |         referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
505 |             t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
506 |       }
507 | 
508 |       if (!referenceAdded) {
509 |         // Don't try to remove a function if we can't inline all
510 |         // the references.
511 |         fs.setRemove(false);
512 |       }
513 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1278 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1279 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1280 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1281 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1282 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1283 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1284 |       } else {
1285 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1286 |       }
1287 |     }
1288 |     return builder.build();
1289 |   }

===== M1018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.aliasAndInlineArguments(Node,LinkedHashMap<String, Node>,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 230-300
Documentation:
/**
   * Inlines the arguments within the node tree using the given argument map,
   * replaces "unsafe" names with local aliases.
   *
   * The aliases for unsafe require new VAR declarations, so this function
   * can not be used in for direct CALL node replacement as VAR nodes can not be
   * created there.
   *
   * @return The node or its replacement.
   */
Method code:
230 |   private Node aliasAndInlineArguments(
231 |       Node fnTemplateRoot, LinkedHashMap<String, Node> argMap,
232 |       Set<String> namesToAlias) {
233 | 
234 |     if (namesToAlias == null || namesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
235 |       // There are no names to alias, just inline the arguments directly.
236 |       Node result = FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(
237 |           compiler, fnTemplateRoot, null, argMap);
238 |       Preconditions.checkState(result == fnTemplateRoot);
239 |       return result;
240 |     } else {
241 |       // Create local alias of names that can not be safely
242 |       // used directly.
243 | 
244 |       // An arg map that will be updated to contain the
245 |       // safe aliases.
246 |       Map<String, Node> newArgMap = Maps.newHashMap(argMap);
247 | 
248 |       // Declare the alias in the same order as they
249 |       // are declared.
250 |       List<Node> newVars = Lists.newLinkedList();
251 |       // NOTE: argMap is a linked map so we get the parameters in the
252 |       // order that they were declared.
253 |       for (Entry<String, Node> entry : argMap.entrySet()) {
254 |         String name = entry.getKey();
255 |         if (namesToAlias.contains(name)) {
256 |           if (name.equals(THIS_MARKER)) {
257 |             boolean referencesThis = NodeUtil.referencesThis(fnTemplateRoot);
258 |             // Update "this", this is only necessary if "this" is referenced
259 |             // and the value of "this" is not Token.THIS, or the value of "this"
260 |             // has side effects.
261 | 
262 |             Node value = entry.getValue();
263 |             if (!value.isThis()
264 |                 && (referencesThis
265 |                     || NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(value, compiler))) {
266 |               String newName = getUniqueThisName();
267 |               Node newValue = entry.getValue().cloneTree();
268 |               Node newNode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(newName, newValue)
269 |                   .copyInformationFromForTree(newValue);
270 |               newVars.add(0, newNode);
271 |               // Remove the parameter from the list to replace.
272 |               newArgMap.put(THIS_MARKER,
273 |                   IR.name(newName)
274 |                       .srcrefTree(newValue));
275 |             }
276 |           } else {
277 |             Node newValue = entry.getValue().cloneTree();
278 |             Node newNode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(name, newValue)
279 |                 .copyInformationFromForTree(newValue);
280 |             newVars.add(0, newNode);
281 |             // Remove the parameter from the list to replace.
282 |             newArgMap.remove(name);
283 |           }
284 |         }
285 |       }
286 | 
287 |       // Inline the arguments.
288 |       Node result = FunctionArgumentInjector.inject(
289 |           compiler, fnTemplateRoot, null, newArgMap);
290 |       Preconditions.checkState(result == fnTemplateRoot);
291 | 
292 |       // Now that the names have been replaced, add the new aliases for
293 |       // the old names.
294 |       for (Node n : newVars) {
295 |         fnTemplateRoot.addChildToFront(n);
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       return result;
299 |     }
300 |   }

===== M1020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 570-642
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
570 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
571 |     if (this == that) {
572 |       return true;
573 |     }
574 | 
575 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
576 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
577 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
578 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
579 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
580 |         // with everyone.
581 |         return true;
582 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
583 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
584 |         // both unknown.
585 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
586 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
587 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
588 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
589 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
590 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
591 |         return false;
592 |       }
593 |     }
594 | 
595 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
596 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
597 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
601 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
602 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
606 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
607 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
608 |     }
609 | 
610 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
611 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
612 |       return false;
613 |     }
614 | 
615 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
616 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
617 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     if (isTemplateType() && that.isTemplateType()) {
621 |       return this == that;
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     // Unbox other proxies.
625 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
626 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
627 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
628 |               that, eqMethod);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
632 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
633 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
634 |           eqMethod);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
638 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
639 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
640 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
641 |     return this == that;
642 |   }

===== M1021 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.mimimizeCost(FunctionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 687-701
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the function is worth inlining and potentially
   * trims references that increase the cost.
   * @return Whether inlining the references lowers the overall cost.
   */
Method code:
687 |   private boolean mimimizeCost(FunctionState fs) {
688 |     if (!inliningLowersCost(fs)) {
689 |       // Try again without Block inlining references
690 |       if (fs.hasBlockInliningReferences()) {
691 |         fs.setRemove(false);
692 |         fs.removeBlockInliningReferences();
693 |         if (!fs.hasReferences() || !inliningLowersCost(fs)) {
694 |           return false;
695 |         }
696 |       } else {
697 |         return false;
698 |       }
699 |     }
700 |     return true;
701 |   }

===== M1026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LineNumberCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LineNumberCheck.java
Original range: 67-80
Documentation:

Method code:
67 |   @Override
68 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
69 |     if (n.isScript()) {
70 |       requiresLineNumbers = false;
71 |     } else if (requiresLineNumbers) {
72 |       if (n.getLineno() == -1) {
73 |         // The tree version of the node is really the best diagnostic
74 |         // info we have to offer here.
75 |         compiler.report(
